## Document 103: 261015_ubs_TSMC

### Page 1

ab 15 October 2026
Global Research
Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing
Full speed on capacity expansion, backed by a
strong US commitment
Cloud AI demand strengthening, with no signs of slowing
TSMC management remains optimistic of a stronger multi-year Cloud AI mega-trend. Its
optimism is based on its prudent and comprehensive assessment of data centre
construction, power shell build up, component supply, and the financial situation of
Cloud service operators. Its capex target was retained at US$60-64bn in 2026, implying

a meaningful uptick to US$19.4-23.4bn in Q4 vs US$40.6bn in 9M26. Our recent
research highlighted TSMC is further accelerating expansion across N3/N2/A14, and
could lift capex to US$90bn in 2027E and US$105bn in 2028E.
Stronger commitment to  US expansion
TSMC guided the overseas dilution on gross margin to rise to 3-4% in 2027 from a
narrowing to 2% (vs. prior 2-3%) in 2026 given the accelerating expansion especially in
the US. The second fab in Arizona should start mass production for N3 in Q327, slightly

earlier than prior plan. Management also stated that it's in talk with SpaceX/Terafab on
potential collaboration, with key principle being to operate the fabs by TSMC.  As what
we wrote recently, TSMC is also evaluating the possibility to expand outside  Arizona,
with Texas being an option. The greater ambition to expand global footprint and
support reshoring is key in solidifying TSMC's competitive positioning, in our view.
Better Q4 sales guidance, slightly down GM guidance not concerning

Q3 EPS of NT$29.43 was above LSEG consensus of NT$28.67, mostly due to higher sales
and OPM of 58.7%. Q3 gross margin of 66.4% was a bit below expectation of 67%
into earnings, given N2 dilution. Q4 sales is guided to grow 14% QoQ at mid point,
slightly ahead of expectation of ~10% QoQ into earnings, driving full year sales
guidance in USD to grow 44% YoY. TSMC guided greater dilution from N2 and overseas
expansion, which lowers Q4 GM 40bps QoQ to 66.0% at the mid point of guidance. We

view TSMC’s investment case on strong growth at sustainable margins with good
support in 2027  (UBS GMs up 10bps YoY at 66.7% from 66.6% in 2026) with price
hikes to pass on higher costs.
Valuation: maintain Buy and target of NT$3,650
We raise 2026-28E EPS by 2-3% to reflect higher sales even with slightly lower gross
margin assumptions. Our new 2028E EPS is 10% above consensus pre-earnings. We
view resilient risk/reward for TSMC stock at 16x 2027E PE vs 36% earnings growth in

2027 based on UBSe, and expect the stock to steadily outperform the broader tech
sector at least through early 2027, assuming the market volatility persists. Reiterate Buy
rating. Our price target is based on 20x 2027-28E PE with a 26% long-term earnings
CAGR.

Company Description
Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing (TSMC) is the world's leading dedicated foundry
service provider, with annual capacity of about 32m of 8-inch equivalent wafers as of end-
2024. It operates four 12-inch wafer fabs, four 8-inch wafer fabs and one 6-inch wafer fab in

Taiwan. TSMC also manages two 8-inch fabs and one 12-inch fab overseas under wholly
owned subsidiaries WaferTech in the US, and TSMC China and TSMC Nanjing in China.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We base our price target for TSMC on a PE multiple.
We think TSMC faces a variety of risks, including rapidly changing technology, intense
competition, high capital investment and cyclical demand. TSMC also has a history of paying
stock options to its employees, which could dilute earnings.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

## Document 104: 261016_daiwa_ Ardentec

Source PDF: all report pdf/261016_daiwa_ Ardentec.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-16
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We preview Ardentec’s 3Q26 results. In our initiation report, 
“Initiation: an emerging force in AI/HPC testing” (Sep 9), we expected 
traditional seasonality in 4Q26-1Q27 to weigh on Ardentec’s capacity 
utilisation. However, we now expect stronger-than-expected momentum in 
the networking segment to drive counter-seasonal resilience through 
1Q27E. We also expect AI revenue (with AI connectivity) contribution to 
exceed 40% in 1Q27E, well ahead of our original expectations.

What’s the impact: Robust networking demand bolsters high 
utilisation and CPO traction. Our industry checks indicate that demand 
for networking products is surging across the testing supply chain, primarily 
fuelled by the acceleration of 1.6T networking adoption. We forecast 
Ardentec’s revenue in 4Q26 and 1Q27 to beat typical seasonal slowdowns. 
As these high-speed networking programs largely follow a customer-
consignment model, their rising revenue mix should provide upside to 
overall gross margin. Concurrently, subsidiary Giga Solution is gaining 
momentum in co-packaged optics (CPO) testing, having already qualified 
and begun ramping up with leading US tier-1 players. While current 
engagement is centred on insertion 1, we see potential for customer base 
diversification and expansion into subsequent test insertions (2 and 3).

3Q26 results preview. With the Longtan fab expansion on track and 
spillover demand continuing, Ardentec 3Q26 revenue reached TWD5.34bn 
(+17.7% QoQ, +48.5% YoY), above the market consensus by 0.8% but 
slightly missing our estimate by 1.4%. With utilisation remaining high in 
3Q26, we estimate its gross margin and operating margin will reach 45.1% 
and 35.5%, respectively, above the consensus by 1.3pp and 1.9pp, 
respectively. We expect cleanroom construction of 2nd and 3rd floors will 
be finalised by year-end 2026 driven by solid domestic AI/HPC orders, with 
2nd-floor capacity likely contributing by late-1Q27.

What we recommend: We raise our 2027-28E EPS by 7% to reflect 
better-than-expected networking demand but lower our 2026E EPS by 1%. 
We reaffirm our Buy (1) rating and raise our 12M TP to TWD395 (from 
TWD340), based on an unchanged target PER of 25x on our 2027E EPS. 
While our target PER represents a premium over the OSAT/testing peer 
average, we believe this premium is justified by steady demand for high-
speed AI connectivity and the Longtan ramp-up. Our estimates indicate 
these factors could lift AI-related revenue to more than 40% of total sales 
by 1Q27. Key downside risk: delays in capacity expansion.

How we differ: Our 2027-28E EPS are 7-26% above the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely on our more aggressive utilisation rate and margin profile.

16 October 2026

3Q26 preview: AI defies seasonality

➢ Solid AI networking demand drives counter-seasonal resilience 
➢ On-track Longtan ramp and CPO upside expand advanced testing 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) rating; raising our 12M TP to TWD395

Company profile 
Founded in 1999, Ardentec is a leading Taiwanese pure-play wafer-testing provider serving foundries, 
IDMs and fabless chipmakers. Its acquisitions of Giga Solution and Raytek over 2016-17 added RF 
testing and bumping capabilities. Combining automotive-grade reliability with flexible AI/HPC capacity, 
the company provides customers a secondary supplier which enhances supply-chain resilience.

Ardentec (3264 TT): 16 October 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 9 Sep 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
The board demonstrates strong governance oversight, with 42% independent directors, regular
performance evaluations, and ESG-linked executive compensation. Governance quality is
further supported by a top 5% ranking in Taiwan’s corporate governance assessment for nine
consecutive years.
Capital management 2
Capital allocation appears disciplined, balancing expansion investments with shareholder
returns. The company funded major capacity growth while maintaining strong profitability and
distributed stable cash dividends, reflecting a prudent and shareholder-friendly capital
management approach.
Related party &
transaction 1
Related-party transactions appear well governed and transparent. The company states that
intercompany dealings follow arm’s-length principles and are not used for tax minimization, while
directors are required to disclose conflicts and abstain from related decisions.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

台新證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市仁愛路四段 118 號 16 樓(02)5589-9558  (113) 金管投顧新字第 021 號 
公司拜訪快報

晟田(4541)：航太及半導體客戶需求延續，帶動 2027 年獲利成長 
建議：中立轉買進

一、晟田為航太精密機械零件加工廠 
晟田成立於 1991 年 8 月，實收資本額 6.76 億元，截至
2Q26 底，員工約 686 位。主要從事航太零組件(包括發
動機機匣與懸塊、起落架)及機械零組件(包括半導體真
空泵浦零組件、食品飲料填充包裝設備零組件、工業自
動化精密傳動零組件等產品)之製造加工與銷售等業務。
2025 年前五大客戶為漢翔(2634)、日本 E 客戶、
SLS(Safran Landing Systems)、SPP (6355.JP)及長榮
航太(2645)。2026 年前 9 個月產品別營收佔比為航太零
組件(客供料) 48%，航太零組件(自購料) 25%，半導體
設備零組件 23%，其他 4%。

二 、 夏季電價及費用認列影響獲利 ， 預估3Q26 稅後 EPS 
1.26 元 
晟田 3Q26 營收 5.6 億元(+1.9%QoQ，+30.9%YoY)，
季增主因為受惠航太客供料客戶拉貨動能成長及半導體
客戶需求提升。獲利方面，儘管產品組合持續優化，惟
受到夏季電價及籌資相關費用認列影響，預估營業利益
1.01 億元(-6%QoQ，+97.1%YoY)。假設匯率持穩，預
估稅後淨利 0.85 億元(-1%QoQ，+34.4%YoY)， 稅後EPS 
1.26 元。 
三、產品組合持續優化，預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 1.3 元 
展望 4Q26，半導體方面，考量客戶需求持續強勁，有
望帶動出貨動能；航太方面，波音(BA.US) 737 系列機
型月產能有望於 2026 年底自 42 架提升至 47 架，另，
根據空中巴士(EADSY.US)規劃，2026 年預計交付 870
架，截至 Sep-26 交付達成率約 6 成，預期 4Q26 將加
速交機 ， 帶動航太零組件拉貨需求 。 整體而言 ， 預估4Q26
營收 5.62 億元(+0.5%QoQ，+20.7%YoY)。獲利方面，
隨產品組合持續優化，且無電價因素干擾，預估營業利
益 1.05 億元(+3.9%QoQ，+28.6%YoY)。 假設匯率持穩，
預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 1.3 元。 
四、需求延續，預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 4.51 元 
展望後市，儘管航太供應鏈仍受原物料短缺、人力及產
能瓶頸影響，惟波音及空巴在手訂單仍維持高位，顯示
終端需求具支撐。隨供應鏈瓶頸逐步緩解，預期將帶動
航太零組件拉貨需求回升，有利晟田營運成長。半導體
方面，受惠終端客戶持續擴大資本支出，帶動相關產品
需求穩定成長。整體而言，預估 2026 年營收 21.2 億元
(+29.1%YoY)。獲利方面，隨產品組合持續優化，預估
毛利率 25.1%，稅後淨利 3.1 億元(+122.9%YoY)，稅後
EPS 4.51 元。 
展望 2027 年，考量航空引擎零組件客戶需求強勁，晟
田規劃擴充相關產能，新廠有望於 2H27 投產；半導體
方面，持續優化相關產能，以因應客戶需求成長。整體
而言，預估 2027 年營收 23.5 億元(+10.7%YoY)。獲利
方面，考量新廠投產後折舊費用增加，預估毛利率年減
至 24.8%， 稅後淨利3.3 億元(+9%YoY)， 稅後EPS 4.58
元(以增資稀釋後股本計算)。 
五、中立轉買進理由 
評價方面，以 2027 年 EPS 估算，目前 PE 約為 13X，
介於晟田歷史區間 12X~20X 下緣 ， 調升至買進評等 ， 目
標價 75 元(2026F PE 17X)。

估 3Q26 稅後 12 億元，季增 15%，年增 27%，EPS 7.67 元 
3Q26進入旺季，營收季增 3%至 496億元，年增 48%，估毛利率季減 0.1
百分點至 19.2%，主因低毛利率子公司帆宣比重提高 0.3百分點及記憶體持
續上漲。估稅後 12億元，季增 15%，年增 27%，EPS 7.6 7元，比原估下
修 5%，主因營收低於原先預期 4%。4Q26 NCR代工訂單/帆宣美國二廠持
續增加，估營收季增 3%，毛利率季持平，稅後 14.3 億元，季增 19%，
EPS 9.1元，比原估上修 8%，主因 Kontron持股由 30%上升至 49%。 
提高 Kontron 持股，有助中長期投資效益 
樺漢提高德國子公司 Kontron AG 持股由原先 29.7%上升至 49.4%(8/20交
割完成 )。影響： 1)財務面：本中心 預期提高持股後， 估計可望提升獲利
10%以上。2)軟硬體與通訊互補：Kontron具有嵌入式運算、5G通訊、資安、
交通及國防航太技術，結合樺漢在邊緣 AI 伺服器、機器人及強固型工業電
腦的硬體優勢，從「單點設備銷售」轉型為「平台化解決方案」。 
上修 2026/27 年獲利 1%/6% 
智慧工廠與廠務(帆宣)目前在手訂單 1,578 億元，MoM+17%，未來成長趨
勢明確。展望 2026~27 年主要工程認列包括台積美國 P2~P3及 AP、高雄
廠 P3~P4、新竹 20廠 P3、台中 18廠 P9等，看好新廠商機，包括設備代
工、代理等，其中封裝設備估計 >3 成之成長。由於認列美國二廠業績加快，
估 2026/27年營收年增 55%/14%至 801/913億元，創新高。 
合計樺漢 2026/27 年營收 1,864/2,104 億元，成長 31%/13%，規模經濟向
上，費用率下滑，估營業利益率 5.57%/5.64%，優於 2025年 3.99%。稅後
44.4/53.6 億元，年增 38%/21%，EPS 2 8.25/34.14 元，比先前預期上修
1%/6%，主因升高子公司 Kontron持股。樺漢受惠提升 Kontron持股及帆宣
認列美國二廠增加，2026/27年營收及獲利高成長，維持買進評等。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
估 4Q26 稅後 14.3 億元，季增 19%，年增 64%，EPS 9. 1 元，比原估
上修 8%，主因 Kontron 持股由 30%上升至 49%。 
⧫  
樺漢將 Kontron  持股由原先 29.7%上升至 49.4%，估計可望提升獲利
10%以上。並從「單點設備銷售」轉型為「平台化解決方案」。 
⧫  
受惠提升 Kontron 持股及帆宣認列美國二廠增加 ，營收及獲利高成
長，維持買進。目標價 545 元係以 2027 年 EPS、16 倍本益比推得。

主因營收低於原先預期 4%

疲弱)。

記憶體漲價大部分轉嫁給客戶，略影響毛利率，但規模經濟向上，營業利益率上揚 
關於記憶體漲價因應，公司已與供應商鎖定全年約 200億元訂單所需之記憶體供貨量，價格雖隨市
場浮動，但會透過長約定價或調價等轉嫁給客戶，若記憶體價格漲幅過大也採客戶 consign 模式，
降低毛利率影響。1Q26 部分客戶接受轉嫁，部分客戶則選擇先觀望遞延專案，造成營收不如預期。
但 2Q26 後客戶已逐漸接受記憶體漲價，出貨恢復正常。由於記憶體轉嫁有落後且難以全數移轉，
加上近期低毛利率的子公司帆宣廠務成長較快，因此估 2026/27 年毛利率 19.4%/19.3%，略低於
2025年 20.5%。但預估 2026/27年整體營收成長 31%/13%，規模經濟明顯向上，費用率下滑，營
業利益率 5.57%/5.64%，遠優於 2025年 3.99%。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026年 10月 19日 Ennoconn 第4 頁，共 19頁  
積極佈局實體 AI 
公司將實體 AI(Physical AI)定義為 AI由數位分析與內容生成，進一步具備感知、理解、決策及實際
執行能力。樺漢過去由 IPC 1.0 工業電腦、IPC 2.0 工業物聯網及 IPC 3.0 智慧平台逐步升級，
2026年開始進一步布局 Physical AI。 
公司建立完整機器人系統工程團隊，涵蓋 AI 感知與理解、機器學習系統、機器人運動控制、嵌入
式運算平台、數位孿生模擬及可靠度設計。 AI 感知包含影像、語音、感測器融合及 VLA（Vision-
Language-Action）技術；模擬驗證採用 NVIDIA Isaac Sim 進行虛擬場景、產線及機器人流程模擬
測試，用以最佳化生產效率。樺漢競爭策略由單純提供板卡與運算模組，轉向提供完整機器人系統
平台。產品布局涵蓋 Sense、Compute、Connect、Control及 Act五大機器人平台零件，應用包括
AMR自主移動機器人、人形機器人、工業製造、智慧物流及商業服務。  
運動控制及機器人相關技術仍在持續投資；模擬驗證軟體毛利率可超過 60%。管理層認為 Physical 
AI 初期仍以研發與平台建置為主，短期商業化重點為工業及商用 AI 應用，人形機器人獲利貢獻仍
需時間。2026年實體 AI為初期發展階段，貢獻樺漢業績約 3億美金，占營收比重 5.2%。長期實
體 AI 於製造場域可協助採購與物流管理、改善長短料問題，並透過即時感測與自動化控制提升工
站品質、精度及生產效率，研調機構預估全球實體 AI產值由 2025年 58億美金，上升至 2035元
產值可達 1200 億美元， CAGR 35.4%。樺漢結合既有智慧工廠與工業物聯網客戶基礎，推動
Physical AI走向實際部署，可望快速成長。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026年 10月 19日 Ennoconn 第5 頁，共 19頁  
圖 8：樺漢機器人與智慧餐飲佈局

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧整理 
提高 Kontron 持股，有助中長期投資效益 
因樺漢集團對 Kontron的總持股比例已來到約 29.72%，接近德國證券法規定的 30%強制公開收購
門檻，樺漢董事會正式授權董事長啟動現金公開收購之前置作業(訂定每股 23.5歐元)。樺漢公開收
購並提高德國子公司 Kontron AG 持股由原先 29.7%上升至 49.4%(8/20交割完成)，核心效益與影
響主要體現在以下幾個面向： 
1)財務面：本中心預期提高持股後，估計可望提升獲利 10%以上(少數股權項目扣除減少)，雙方深
化整合後，透過樺漢母公司鴻海製造資源，預期從 2027 年開始全面發酵具體的營收與成本規模經
濟效益。

### Page 6

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026年 10月 19日 Ennoconn 第6 頁，共 19頁  
2)業務與技術整合(實體 AI 平台化) 
• 軟硬體與通訊互補：Kontron具有嵌入式運算、5G通訊、資安、交通及國防航太技術，結合樺漢
在邊緣 AI 伺服器、機器人及強固型工業電腦的硬體優勢，從「單點設備銷售」轉型為「平台化解
決方案」。 
• 加速跨場景落地：有助於將無人結帳、智慧零售、 AMR（自主移動機器人）及工廠自動化等應用
標準化，降低客戶導入門檻，並加快在北美及歐洲市場的開案與交付效率。  
Kontron 成長領域包括交通運輸、國防航太、 5G/FRMCS/NAD，以及軟體與解決方案，營收目標
由 2026年 16億歐元上升至 2030年 26億歐元(CAGR 13%)、EBITDA達 4.2億歐元，並藉由提高
平台及服務收入占比改善獲利結構。

結盟 NCR 增加 ODM 訂單 2Q26 開始出貨，有助工業物聯網部門成長 
樺漢與 NCR Voyix (股票代號：VYX US)結盟，該公司是全球消費者技術的領導者，主要生產 POS、
自動櫃員機、條碼閱讀器等，提供高品質的技術解決方案，並幫助企業在快速變化的數位環境中 蓬
勃發展。NCR Voyix 在市場領先的軟體和服務與樺漢的硬體專業知識技術高度互補，並有助樺漢
ESaaS及 ODM業務進一步擴展，為雙贏的結盟。 
NCR年營收約 1,230億元，其中硬體占比 32%，軟體 68%，硬體部分約 400億元台幣訂單未來全
交給樺漢代工，其中 160億元為新接訂單[NCR之前即為樺漢 ODM客戶，60%硬體(約 240億元)
交樺漢生產]，此部分將貢獻在工業物聯網 (含 Kontron、ODM)業務上，2025 年因應川普關稅議題，
NCR IT 全球性服務需調整期，發展整合系統；樺漢墨西哥製造基地調整至田納西。因此原本增加
NCR代工訂單由 10月再度延至 2026年 1月出貨，後因美伊戰爭及 DRAM漲價影響，客戶延至
2Q26正式出貨。受惠此訂單增加，估 2026/27 年工業物聯網營收成長 24%/13%至 845/958億元。

智慧工廠與廠務(帆宣)進入美國二廠密集認列期，營運創新高 
智慧工廠與廠務(帆宣)目前在手訂單 1,578億元，MoM+17%，主要來自晶圓代工廠、記憶體廠擴
廠積極，另外新增資料中心建置，半導體供應鏈普遍面臨交期、產能壓力，加上具有能力承接大型
專案廠商有限，使得廠務接近賣方市場，未來 2~3 年成長趨勢更為明確。展望 2026~27 年主要
工程認列包括台積美國 P2~P3及 AP、高雄廠 P3~P4、新竹 20廠 P3、台中 18廠 P9等，並且看
好新廠商機，包括設備代工、代理等，其中封裝設備估計 >3 成之成長。智慧工廠與廠務(帆宣) 認
列美國二廠業績加快，2026 年前 9 月營收成長 56%至 574 億元，第四季旺季營收持續向上，估
2026/27年營收年增 55%/14%至 801/913億元，創新高。 
2026/27 年年營收及獲利維持高成長 
智慧軟體方案策略進入 IPC 3.0Ⅱ階段，推廣工控/智慧平台，未來專注於雲端+邊緣 AI與大數據外，
亦提升工業級語言模型與機器人技術，聚焦產業包括：國防、航太、交通、零售、建築等。產品包
括：Edge AI Server & Device、Token、AI Agent、Physical AI等，公司積極佈局中，近期因專案
認列延後，估 2026 年營收 218 億元，衰退 3%。2027 年代理/實體 AI 發酵，估營收成長 7%至
233億元。 
合計樺漢 2026/27年營收 1,864/2,104億元(下修 2%/2%，主因智慧軟體方案營收低於預期)，成長
31%/13%，低毛利率的智慧工廠與廠務(帆宣)成長較大及上游原物料 Dram大漲，估 2026/27年毛

### Page 8

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026年 10月 19日 Ennoconn 第8 頁，共 19頁  
產業概況  
工業電腦未來 CAGR 6%，為下游成長穩定的產業 
工業電腦在 2000 年後網路發達，亦造成工業電腦用途日漸擴大，由工廠發展至生活端。過去常受
全球製造業景氣影響，後因應用面變廣，成長性加快。 
工業電腦近年成長率在 5~10%，是下游成長穩定的產業。根據研調機構統計及預估，工業電腦
2023年產值約為 45億美元，可望增加至 2037年的 96億美元，CAGR 6%比一般 PC低個位數成
長為佳，主因公司對工業物聯網、製造業數位化、邊緣 AI 建置…等因素推動，其中半導體、製造
業、汽車、物流、零售…等均為重要成長來源。 
圖 9：工業電腦 2023-2037 年產值 CAGR 6%

資料來源：Markets and Markets、元大投顧整理 
工業 4.0 帶動物聯網需求大增 
從 18世紀末經歷多次工業生產變革，20世紀末進入 21世紀，工業製造由 3.0發展至 4.0，其中最
大改變就是組裝、加工、搬運 …等由原先自動化機台 (工業 3.0)，加入智能回饋，透過物聯網收集
巨量資料，傳輸至雲端運算，統計分析後，即時改善生產製程。隨時因應機台老舊、材料或周遭環
境變化，自動調整量產系統。 
第四次工業革命和過去相比，網路無所不在，移動性提高；感測器體積變得更小、性能更強大、成
本也更低。與此同時，人工智慧和機器學習也開始嶄露鋒芒，相關應用不斷增加。

### Page 9

圖 10：歷次工業變革

資料來源：元大投顧整理 
預估人工智慧物聯網(AIoT)2025~2034 年 CAGR 達 22.16% 
物聯網基礎架構分為五層：物件、通訊與網路、運算與儲存、應用與服務、分析與統計。台灣工業
電腦公司較專注在前端物件 (感測元件)以及中端的運算與儲存二項業務。尤其前端的感測元件為台
灣強項，諸如：POS 機、ATM、機器人手臂、安控攝影機…等。近年受惠人工智慧(AI)導入，成長
加快。 
全球人工智慧物聯網(AIoT)未來受惠於 Data Center、AI Server 建置、企業數位轉型…等因素穩定
成長，研調機構預估 AIoT 2025~2034 年 CAGR 22.16%，為未來工業電腦成長重心。人工智慧物
聯網(AIoT)在全球使用廣泛，目前工業電腦產業使用方面已經陸續切入智慧家庭、車聯網、自動化
設備、機器人…等領域。

公司簡介  
樺漢專精於工業電腦硬體解決方案。終端應用包括 POS機、銀行自動化、互動式資訊服務系統、彩券機及工業自動化。產
品組合方面(2025)，工業物聯網 48%、智慧工廠與廠務 36%、智慧軟體與方案 16%。銷售區域 (2025)北美 26%、歐洲
37%、中國 9%、其他 28%。主要生產交由母公司鴻海代工，另外樺漢在歐洲新設匈牙利自有工廠，取得當地娛樂、金融
零售、工業網安等訂單，維繫 ODM業務成長。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：工業電腦 2026年 10月 19日 Ennoconn 第16 頁，共 19頁

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 樺漢整體的 ESG風險評級屬於低風險，於 Sustainalytics資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排名亦排行於領先的位置，
在科技硬體行業中的公司中排名略為領先同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 樺漢的整體曝險屬於中等水準，略高於科技硬體行業的平均水準。公司較需要改善的  ESG 議
題包含公司資安、商業道德、人力資源等。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：樺漢已採取若干措施來管理與重大 ESG議題相關的風險，但這些措施尚不足以涵蓋
其所面臨的所有風險。儘管如此，該公司並未捲入具有重大財務影響的風險事件。  
 圖 27：ESG 分析

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

## Document 153: 6467泰合 永豐

### Page 1

個股聚焦  
2026/10/20

陳奕均 fion.chen@sinopac.com

泰合 (6467 TT) 
開花結果在即

永豐觀點 
抗血栓口溶膜貼片 BEQIA 歐美取證在即。 
 投資評價與建議 
投資建議買進：泰合 10/20 上市掛牌，掛牌價105.6 元。由於(1) 泰合開發之口溶
膜劑型藥物有效解決高齡與吞嚥困難之臨床痛點下，市場競爭力足；(2) 抗血栓口
溶膜貼片 BEQIA 歐美取證在即，2027 年在授權與上市權利金開始認列下 ，營運轉
虧為盈且呈大跳躍狀；(3) 由於多數學名藥廠受限專利訴訟，最快需至 2028 年 4
月後才可上市銷售，促使泰合享有約一年的「黃金市場空窗期」 ，能與原廠直接瓜
分市場。且因 BEQIA 屬 505(b)(2)新劑型新藥，即便2Q28 後學名藥陸續進場，其
獨特的劑型優勢仍能築起價格防護牆，所面臨的價折壓力相對輕微，建議買進，目
標價 170 元( 18 X 2027 EPS )。

營運現況與分析 
公司簡介：泰合生技成立於 2010 年，專注開發 505(b)(2)新劑型新藥與特殊學名
藥，核心技術為自主研發的 TDS 透皮／透黏膜傳輸平台，將現有口服藥品成分改
造為透皮與透黏膜藥物傳導劑型 ，提供方便使用且更安全的新劑型藥物。 產品開發
上，505(b)(2)部分，治療抗血栓口溶膜貼片(每日兩片)BEQIA 靜待歐美取證中外，
其餘則仍在臨床階段中，困難學名藥部分，化療止吐口溶膜2018 年日本取證銷售
中外，其餘四項藥品則在進行洽談授權合作。 
圖一：泰合產品線開發時程

資料來源：泰合；永豐投顧研究部整理，Oct. 2026

### Page 2

2

口溶膜劑型藥物能有效解決高齡與吞嚥困難之臨床痛點 ：傳統口服錠劑藥物必須經
過食道、進入胃腸道消化，再經由肝臟代謝後才進入血液循環，泰合開發之口溶膜
劑型藥物， 可讓藥膜在接觸舌頭或口腔黏膜數秒內快速溶解 ， 病患完全不需飲水即
可完成給藥， 且部份藥物分子能直接透過口腔黏膜上皮細胞微血管吸收， 直接進入
全身血液循環， 不僅能提高藥物生體可用率， 還能減少胃酸對藥物的破壞與對胃腸
道的刺激下，有效解決高齡與吞嚥困難之臨床痛點，提高患者服藥順從性。 
抗血栓口溶膜貼片 BEQIA 歐美取證在即，提前卡位下，營運動能可期：泰合自主
開發的抗血栓口溶膜新劑型新藥 BEQIA， 預計4Q26 取得歐盟藥證 、2Q27 獲美國
FDA 核准上市。目前該藥原廠在美國市場年銷售額>200 億美元，相較原廠口服錠
劑，泰合 BEQIA 具備三大競爭優勢：不需飲水（服藥便利）、解決吞嚥困難（減
輕照顧端壓力），以及攜帶與給藥便利（優化長照與醫療機構給藥流程）。 
市場競爭方面，由於多數學名藥廠受限專利訴訟，最快需至2028 年 4 月後才可上
市銷售，促使泰合享有約一年的「黃金市場空窗期」 ，能與原廠直接瓜分市場。此
外，由於BEQIA 屬 505(b)(2)新劑型新藥，即便2Q28 後學名藥陸續進場，其獨特
的劑型優勢仍能築起價格防護牆，所面臨的折價壓力應相對輕微。 
泰合海外市場營銷預計將採授權分潤模式，研究部預估 1H27 歐美地區簽約金應
有機會各達 500~1000 萬美金外，隨美國地區 2Q27 上市、通路鋪設及醫保陸續
准入，Peak sales 市佔率 2-3%(預期將落於 2029-30 年)、銷售分潤 12-15%估算
下，稅後 EPS 貢獻將落於 10-20 元。 
財務預估：預估泰合 2026 年營收 0.1 億元，稅後虧損 1.89 億元，稅後 EPS 以掛
牌後股本 5.99 億元估算，稅後 EPS-3.15 元，2027 年預估歐美地區授權簽約金各
500 萬美金及 1000 萬美金 ， 及上市銷售後權利金認列下 ，預估營收將跳升至7.75
億元，稅後淨利 5.68 億元，稅後 EPS 9.49 元。

泰合(6467)

承銷價:105.6 
興櫃價(低/高)： 
32.25/302

Target Price:260

泰合主力產品 BEQIA 專為吞嚥困難族群設計 ， 以Apixaban 為成分
的抗血栓口溶膜，鎖定高齡、吞嚥困難及需長期抗血栓治療族群 。
BEQIA 於 2026 年 6 月 22 日重新送件美國 FDA，進入 10 個月
PDUFA 審查期，2027 年 4 月 22 日為審核完成目標日期，可望於
2Q27 取得美國暫時性核准(Tentative Approval)。 歐洲EMA 方面 ，
則於 2026 年 7 月 17 日完成 Day 121 關鍵清單回覆 ， 朝CHMP 最
終審查意見(Day 210)與 EC 核准推進，公司預計 4Q26 可望獲歐盟
上市許可 。授權策略方面，泰合採取原廠與非原廠雙軌並行 。 對原廠
而言，BEQIA 可借力其成熟通路快速推升口溶模市占，並在面臨專
利懸崖與學名藥競爭時，可藉此維持利潤表現，洽談時即以解決原
廠現有困境的定位切入 。 至於非原廠 ， 泰合採精準區域分擊 ， 鎖定主
要市場以外的區域優先展開談判，累積市場實績與聲量。 泰合目前
已在歐美、紐澳、拉丁美洲等地區與數十家藥廠簽訂NDA，並持續
洽談，多家跨國藥廠與各區域BD 代表已進入實質洽談階段。此外，
下一代產品 TAH3341 長效口溶膜雖仍處臨床前，但也有廠商表達
興趣並關注發展進度 。泰合對外競價拍賣 5100 張 ， 競拍底價88 元，
最高投標張數 669 張，每股承銷價 105.6 元。評價上，參考同業有
6576 逸達以及 6838 台新藥，逸達生技近年股價區間 58-141 元，
股本 15.75 億元，市值區間 91.3-222 億元。台新藥近年股價區間
22-70 元，股本 15.1 億元，市值區間 33-106 億元。而泰合目前股
本 6 億元，參考同業市值區間，給予目標價 260 元，給予買進之投
資建議。

➢ 公司簡介： 
泰合生技成立於 2010 年 7 月，為新劑型新藥及特殊學名藥開發廠，藉由自
行開發之藥物傳輸平台技術結合現有藥物成份，將現有口服藥品成分改造為
透皮與透黏膜藥物傳導劑型，以經皮吸收貼片與口溶膜藥品為代表 。公司專
注於開發 505(b)(2)NDA 新劑型新藥，並藉由 Transepithelial Delivery 
System (TDS)藥物傳輸技術平台改良現有藥物缺點，主要包括透皮及透黏膜
兩種不同藥物傳輸平台，劑型主要包含經皮吸收貼片、口溶膜與口頰溶膜三
類。產品線涵蓋抗血栓藥品、鴉片過量解毒及戒毒劑、小兒過動症、化療止
吐等治療領域。截至 2025 年，營收來自已授權之學名藥及受委託開發之專
案收入 99%。

抗凝血藥物市場: 
在全球醫療環境中，心血管疾病為主要致死與致殘原因之一，其發生率隨人
口老齡化及現代生活型態改變而持續上升。相關疾病涵蓋冠心病、高血壓及
高血脂等，並可能進一步導致心肌梗塞與中風等重大臨床事件。根據世界衛
生組織（WHO）統計，約有五分之四之心血管疾病死亡與心臟病發作及中
風相關，顯示其對全球公共健康之重大影響。

在臨床治療上，抗凝血藥物為心血管疾病管理中不可或缺之核心用藥之一，
主要用於預防及控制血栓形成，以降低心肌梗塞、中風及其他嚴重併發症之
風險。隨著民眾對心血管健康意識提升，以及疾病預防需求增加，抗凝血藥
物之臨床應用持續擴大，成為全球醫療體系中重要之基礎治療。 
在市場規模方面，全球抗凝血藥物市場於 2025 年約為 414.1 億美元，預計將
持續成長至 2034 年約 970.9 億美元 ， 年複合成長率約為9.93%， 顯示該市場

泰合產品介紹: 
➢ BEQIA(TAH3311 Apixaban ODF)抗血栓口溶膜: 
TAH3311(BEQIA)是全球首創的抗血栓口溶膜新劑型新藥，以 BMS/Pfizer 開
發的 Eliquis 抗血栓錠劑為對照藥物。Eliquis 有效成分為 Apixaban，透過直
接抑制 Xa 凝血因子來治療和預防靜脈血栓 ，根據 BMS 財報 ，2025 年 Eliquis
全球銷售額達 144 億美元，其中美國及美國以外之銷售額分別達 102 億美元
和 42 億美元。Eliquis 佔 BMS 總營收約 29.8%，Eliquis 原料藥專利於 2028
年 10 月 17 日到期，劑型專利於 2031 年 8 月 24 日到期，為 505(b)(2)新劑
型新藥創造良好的切入時點，專利懸崖帶來營收的巨大挑戰，原廠已和解的幾
家學名藥廠預計最快將於 2028 年 4 月上市。Eliquis 除增加小兒用的粉劑外 ，
未開發出新劑型，而研發新一代的 FXIa 抗血栓藥數據臨床三期試驗療效及安
全性數據仍待觀察 ，並且抗凝血錠劑在具有吞嚥困難患者 使用痛點仍無法解
決。 
圖四：2Q26 Eliquis 營收及 2026 年財測

資料來源：泰合、第一金投顧整理

表一：2021 年後原廠 BMS/Pfizer 與 Apixaban 學名藥廠 P4 訴訟和解狀況

TAH3311(BEQIA)為泰合重點開發產品，其主要成分為 Apixaban，並採用口
溶膜（orodispersible film, ODF）劑型設計。Apixaban 透過選擇性抑制凝
血因子 Xa，可有效降低中風與全身性血栓風險，而降低非瓣膜性心房顫動
(NVAF)成年患者發生中風和全身性栓塞的風險是最大的應用，佔 Eliquis 總
銷售額的 70%。BEQIA 口溶膜鎖定改善高齡患者吞嚥困難的用藥痛點，相較
於 Eliquis 是一天吃兩錠需要搭配喝水，BEQIA 是一天吃兩片，僅需放入口
中即可快速溶解完成用藥，不須喝水、沒有噎嗆風險，優化長照與醫療機構
給藥流程。2025 年美國有 1,055 萬名心房顫動(AF)患者，其中約 75%之
791 萬名患者為 65 歲以上高齡患者，這當中 50~75%有吞嚥困難，故
BEQIA 在美國目標病患族群達 395.5 萬~593 萬人，隱含目標市場規模
110~164 億美元。

資料來源：泰合、第一金投顧整理

圖八：BEQIA 授權模式: 原廠與非原廠雙軌並行

資料來源：泰合、第一金投顧整理

➢ TAH3341 長效劑型: 
在抗凝血治療市場中 ， 患者多為高齡且需長期甚至終生用藥族群 ， 臨床上普遍
存在多重用藥及吞嚥困難等問題，進而影響用藥便利性與治療依從性。儘管
Apixaban（Eliquis®）已為目前凝血因子 Xa 抑制劑中之領導產品， 
具備良好療效與安全性 ， 其每日兩次給藥之頻率及傳統錠劑劑型 ， 對部分患者
而言仍存在使用負擔 ， 顯示在既有標準治療基礎上 ， 透過劑型與給藥方式創新
以提升依從性，仍具明確之未被滿足醫療需求。 
TAH3341 為泰合延伸既有 Apixaban 產品線所開發之長效抗血栓口溶膜產
品，結合口溶膜（orodispersible film, ODF ）與先進緩釋技術，將原需每日
兩次服用之錠劑優化為 「每日一次」 之創新劑型。產品可於口腔中迅速崩解並
釋放藥物，使患者無需吞嚥或飲水即可完成給藥；同時，透過精密設計之控釋
機制 ， 使有效成分於體內持續穩定釋放 ， 維持適當血中濃度並達成長時間抗凝
效果。 此一 「快速崩解× 延長釋放」 之雙重設計，有效兼顧給藥便利性與療效
穩定性，並有助於降低血中濃度波動所帶來之潛在風險。 
在產品定位上，TAH3341 於維持 Apixaban 既有療效與安全性基礎上，進一
步透過降低給藥頻次與改善劑型使用方式 ， 顯著提升患者用藥依從性與長期治
療持續性。特別針對高齡族群、吞嚥困難患者及多重用藥族群，此一劑型可有
效降低給藥障礙並優化治療體驗。同時，產品亦有助於簡化用藥流程，降低照
護者負擔並提升整體用藥管理效率。TAH3341 透過 「標準治療藥物× 長效化
設計× 創新劑型平台」之整合策略，不僅回應現有抗凝血治療在依從性與便
利性方面之未被滿足需求 ， 亦進一步強化產品差異化與臨床價值 ， 有望於持續
成長之抗血栓市場中建立具競爭力之產品定位。

## Document 164: 9月财政数据跟踪：财政支出有望继续提速

Source PDF: all report pdf/9月财政数据跟踪：财政支出有望继续提速.pdf
Report source: 万联证券
Date: 2026-10-26
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

[Table_RightTitle] 
宏观研究|宏观跟踪报告

[Table_Title] 
财政支出有望继续提速

[Table_ReportType] 
——9 月财政数据跟踪

[Table_ReportDate] 2026 年 10 月 26 日

[T able_Summary] 
⚫ 事件：1-9 月，全国一般性公共预算收入当月同比增速-
0.12%，上月值为4.68%。全国一般性公共预算支出当月同
比增速0.03%，前值为-0.13%。 
⚫ 财政收入当月同比转为负增，土地相关税种对地方财政拖
累持续。9 月，税收、非税收入增速均持续放缓。中央财
政收入下滑速度加快，地方财政收入持续回落。契税跌幅
扩大，房产税边际上行。地方政府土地出让收入累计同比
为-27.9%，土地增值税增速由正转负，耕地占用税跌幅加
深，城镇土地使用税增速也明显下滑。 
⚫ 国内增值税为主要拉动，消费税跌幅收窄，出口退税大幅
增长给税收收入带来拖累。企业所得税同比回落，原材料
价格上涨给企业盈利带来一定压力，个人所得税同比下行
斜率放缓。工业和服务业生产改善，工业品价格上行，国
内增值税上行。必选消费边际修复，9 月1 日起对部分电
池开征消费税政策落地，国内消费税同比跌幅收窄。出口
保持强劲，关税增速上行，外贸出口退税增速由-1.7%大
幅拉升至29%。9 月股票市场震荡回落，成交额缩量，叠
加去年同期高基数（+342.4%）影响，证券交易印花税增
速转负。 
⚫ 财政支出提速，但进程依然偏缓。财政支出当月同比增速
转正，但累计同比持续回落。从投放进程来看，9 月边际
提速，整体进展仍低于近几年均值。中央财政支出高位小
幅放缓，仍为主要支撑；地方支出小幅修复上行，但地方
化债和土地收入约束下，地方财政依然承压。 
⚫ 民生类支出回落，基建内部分化，科技支撑高位边际放
缓。民生方面，社会保障和就业、卫生健康回落幅度较
大，文旅传媒、教育增速上行。民生类支出拨付节奏前
置，单月增速放缓。科学技术增速高位回落，但仍为政策
重点方向。基建方面，交通运输、农林水事务增速下跌，
而城乡社区事务同比增速大幅上行，地方化债和项目储备
不足，传统基建改善不均衡。债务付息增速转负，部分受
付息节奏错位和基数抬升影响，对文旅传媒等非刚性财政
支出的压减节奏有所放缓。但当前债务负担依然较大，付
息压力仍难缓解。 
⚫ 政府性基金收入小幅修复上行，土地财政退坡约束下，地
方财政支出依然受限。地产销售“金九”行情表现疲弱，
房企资金来源受限，土地市场景气度持续偏低。土地财政

退坡仍是地方财政支出的主要约束。全国政府性基金支出
累计同比增速由-17%回升至-16.2%，随着9 月政府债发行
提速，增速逐步修复，但资金到位形成实物工作量仍存在
时滞，四季度有望持续修复。

进程仍有待进一步提速，财政资金向基建支出边际倾斜，
新型政策性金融工具和地方政府债务结存限额将给四季度
基建带来资金支持， “六张网”建设将为重点方向。财政
土地出让收入缩减的拖累仍将持续，四季度仍需关注政策
工具落地进程 
⚫ 风险因素：居民消费转弱，地缘政治风险发展超预期，地
产投资回落幅度超预期。

万联证券研究所 www.wlzq.cn 第 4 页 共 8 页  
事件 
2026年1-9月，全国一般性公共预算收入累计同比由5.7%回落至5.1%，当月同比增速
-0.12%，上月值为4.68%。全国一般性公共预算支出累计同比由1.2%回落至1%，当月
同比增速0.03%，前值为-0.13%。

1    财政收入持续回落，出口退税拖累明显 
财政收入当月同比转为负增，完成预算目标进程依然较快。1-9月，全国一般公共预
算收入累计同比由5.7%回落至5.1%，当月同比增速-0.12%，年内首次出现负增，前值
为4.68%。从收入节奏来看，1-9月累计完成全年预算收入目标的78.07%， 仍处于近五
年同期的高点。居民信贷需求放缓， 加杠杆意愿不足， 内需依然偏弱。企业生产保持
旺盛，但成本端压力抬升和内需偏弱的影响下，企业投资扩产相对谨慎。 
中央财政收入下滑速度加快， 地方财政收入持续回落， 土地相关税种对地方财政拖累
持续。9月， 中央财政收入同比增速由9.27%回落至0.18%， 地方财政收入由0.71%下行
至-0.34%， 地方财政收入依然承压。 房产税当月同比由0%上行至2.92%,契税跌幅扩大。
地方政府土地出让收入9月累计同比由-28.6%小幅收窄为-27.9%，土地增值税增速由
正转负，耕地占用税跌幅加深，城镇土地使用税增速也明显下滑。

图表1：财政收入当月同比增速转负（%） 图表2：公共财政收入各月进度（%）

资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所 资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所

非税、税收收入增速均持续放缓。1-9月， 税收收入累计同比增速由6.6%回落至6.1%，
9月当月同比增速由5.6%下滑至0.2%。 非税收入累计同比增速由1.5%小幅回落至1.2%，
当月同比增速由0.3%下滑至-1%。 
各税种表现分化，国内增值税为主要拉动， 消费税跌幅收窄， 外需强劲拉动相关税种
表现，出口退税大幅增长给税收收入带来拖累。分税种来看，9月，企业所得税同比
增速由11.6%回落至-0.4%， 原材料价格上涨给企业盈利带来一定压力， 个人所得税同
比由11.8%回落至10.5%，下行斜率放缓。工业和服务业生产改善，工业品价格上行，
国内增值税由4%上涨至7.3%。必选消费边际修复，9月1日起对部分电池开征消费税政
策落地，国内消费税同比跌幅由-14%收窄至-8.5%。出口保持强劲，外贸相关税种表

2    财政支出增速转正，地方财力偏紧制约支出扩张 
财政支出当月同比增速转正，投放提速， 但支出进程依然偏缓， 地方财政支出小幅修
复。2026年9月，全国一般性公共预算支出累计同比由1.2%回落至1%，当月同比增速
0.03%，前值为-0.13%。从投放进程来看，达到全年预算的70.04%，低于近几年同期
水平。9月投放进程边际提速，但支出整体进展依然偏慢。四季度财政支出仍有较大
发力空间。中央财政支出当月同比增速由4.7%下行至4.6%,地方财政支出由-1.1%收窄
至-0.8%,地方财政支出增速上行， 但地方化债和土地收入约束下， 地方财政依然承压。

万联证券研究所 www.wlzq.cn 第 6 页 共 8 页  
政府性基金收入小幅修复上行，土地财政退坡约束下，地方财政支出依然受限。1-9
月，全国政府性基金收入累计同比增速由-19%继续上升至-18.6%， 地方本级政府性基
金收入累计同比增速由 -22.9%回升至-22.3%。地方土地出让收入累计同比增速由 -
28.6%收窄至-27.9%，地产销售“金九”行情表现疲弱，房企资金来源受限，土地市
场景气度持续偏低。 土地财政退坡仍是地方财政支出的主要约束。全国政府性基金支
出累计同比增速由-17%回升至-16.2%，随着9月政府债发行提速，增速逐步修复，但
资金到位形成实物工作量仍存在时滞，四季度有望持续修复。 
民生类支出节奏前置， 单月增速放缓， 基建内部分化， 科技高位边际放缓。分支出类
型看，9月，民生方面，社会保障和就业当月同比由2.6%下跌至-4.8%,卫生健康也回
落幅度较大，文旅传媒增速由-9.6%上行至1.6%，教育上行2.2个百分点至3.5%,节能
环保同比修复上行， 但增速仍处低位。 民生类支出拨付节奏前置， 单月增速放缓。科
学技术增速放缓， 但仍为政策重点方向。 基建类支出内部分化加剧， 其中，交通运输
跌幅进一步加深， 农林水事务增速由正转负， 城乡社区事务同比增速大幅上行，地方
化债和项目储备不足，传统基建改善不均衡。 
债务付息增速转负，但并非趋势性拐点。9月债务付息当月同比增速由7%降至-1.2%,
部分受付息节奏错位和基数抬升影响 ，对文旅传媒等非刚性财政支出的压减节奏有
所放缓。但当前债务负担依然较大，付息压力仍难缓解。

3    财政赤字季节性走扩 
财政赤字季节性走扩。1-9月，财政盈余由上月的24,818亿元上行至37,907亿元，赤
字水平较去年同期已经有所回落。 
四季度财政支持力度加大， 支出有望迎来提速。财政支出进程仍有待进一步提速，财
政资金向基建支出边际倾斜，新型政策性金融工具和地方政府债务结存限额将给四
季度基建带来资金支持， “六张网”建设将为重点方向。财政土地出让收入缩减的拖
累仍将持续，四季度仍需关注政策工具落地进程

4    风险提示 
居民消费转弱，地缘政治风险发展超预期，地产投资回落幅度超预期。

[Table_InudstryRankInfo] 行业投资评级 
强于大市：未来6个月内行业指数相对大盘涨幅10％以上； 
同步大市：未来6个月内行业指数相对大盘涨幅10％至-10％之间； 
弱于大市：未来6个月内行业指数相对大盘跌幅10％以上。

[Table_StockRankInfo] 公司投资评级 
买入：未来6个月内公司相对大盘涨幅15％以上； 
增持：未来6个月内公司相对大盘涨幅5％至15％； 
观望：未来6个月内公司相对大盘涨幅-5％至5％； 
卖出：未来6个月内公司相对大盘跌幅5％以上。 
基准指数 ：沪深300指数

[Table_RiskInfo] 风险提示 
我们在此提醒您， 不同证券研究机构采用不同的评级术语及评级标准。 我们采用的是相对评级体系， 表示投资的
相对比重建议；投资者买入或者卖出证券的决定取决于个人的实际情况，比如当前的持仓结构以及其他需要考
虑的因素。投资者应阅读整篇报告，以获取比较完整的观点与信息，不应仅仅依靠投资评级来推断结论。

[Table_PromiseInfo] 证券分析师承诺

出具本报告。 本报告清晰准确地反映了本人的研究观点。 本人不曾因， 不因， 也将不会因本报告中的具体推荐意
见或观点而直接或间接收到任何形式的补偿。

## Document 192: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적HD현대중공업(329180) 우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적HD현대중공업(329180) 우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung

### Page 1

HD 현대중공업 (329180/KS) 
우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적 
2026-10-14

조선/방산. 한승한, R.A 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 820,000 원(하향) 
현재주가: 366,500 원 
상승여력: 123.7%

Signal: 3Q26, 조업일수 및 환율 영향으로 시장 예상치 하회 예상 
Key: 4Q26부터 본격적인 발전엔진 및 FDC 계약 수주 모멘텀 기대 
Step: 상선 우려 감안해도 4 행정 엔진&FDC으로 주가 상승여력 
충분. 매수

3Q26 Preview: 컨센서스 하회하는 실적 기록 예상 
3Q26 연결 매출액 6조 464억원(+36.9% YoY, -4.5% QoQ), 영업이익 9,230억
원(+65.6% YoY, -11.2% QoQ)으로, 시장 예상치(1조 70억원)를 하회하는 실적 기
록할 것으로 추정한다. 이전 분기 대비 중대재해 및 하계휴가, 그리고 추석 연휴에 따른 
조업일수 감소 효과와 더불어 큰 폭의 환율 하락 영향(약 500억원) 때문이다. 홍범도
함 화재 및 임단협 관련 일회성 비용은 이번 분기가 아닌 4Q 에 반영될 것으로 예상한
다. 해양 부문에서 트리온 FPU 프로젝트 C/O에 따른 일부 환입 발생한 것으로 추정
되며, 해양 도크에서 컨테이너선 선체 블록 일부 건조로 고정비 감소효과 일부 상쇄 중
이다. 다만 올해 연내 혹은 내년 초 추가 해양 수주 없을 시 고정비 상승 우려 확대될 
전망이다. 동사 올해 상선 수주 목표(114.7억불)를 이미 초과하는 수주 실적 기록한 상
황이며, 고마진 선종인 LNGC 및 VLGC/VLAC 중심으로 수주했기 때문에 ‘28~’29
년 구간 경쟁사 대비 뚜렷한 선종 믹스 효과를 기대한다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 82 만원으로 하향 
HD 현대중공업에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’를 유지하고, 목표주가는 기존 97 만원에서 82
만원으로 하향한다. 중국의 공격적인 캐파 증설 지속과 더불어 중국 대형 조선사들의 
글로벌 선주가 발주한 LNGC 물량 및 원가 경쟁력 확대에 따른 상선 부문의 멀티플 
조정이 불가피하다는 판단이다. 하지만 상선 부문의 우려를 감안하더라도 상선 사이클
과 무관한 육상 발전 엔진 및 FDC에 대한 글로벌 하이퍼스케일러들의 수요 확대는 지
속되고 있는 중으로 파악된다. 올해 4 분기부터 4 행정 중속엔진&FDC 부문의 본격적
인 수주를 통해 중장기적 신성장 동력 확보할 전망이며, 해당 모멘텀에 따른 주가 상승
여력 충분히 남아있다는 판단으로, 조선주 최선호주(Top-Pick)를 유지한다.

본 보고서에 언급된 종목의 경우 당사 조사분석담당자는 본인의 담당종목을 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
본 보고서는 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공된 사실이 없습니다. 
당사는 자료공표일 현재 해당기업과 관련하여 특별한 이해 관계가 없습니다. 
종목별 투자의견은 다음과 같습니다.

Editorial 
A Fed credibility-induced bounce  
Strategy summary:  The Fed’s unanimous decision to hike 
rates this week for the first time since 2023 , and pencil in 
another hike by December, confirms our expectations – flagged 
in the last two weeklies - of a central bank willing to establish its 
independence against political pressure. The Fed’s moderately 
hawkish pivot is helping restore its inflation-fighting credentials. 
The BoJ also hiked rates this week. It is the first time on record 
all three major central banks – Fed, ECB and BoJ – have hiked 
in the same month , as they respond primarily to a resurgence 
in energy-driven inflation amid tight job markets.  
We expect the Fed to hike once more by year-end to 4.0-4.25% 
and follow up with another hike in H1 2027 to 4.25 -4.5%. With 
the revised estimates, we revise up our US government bond 
yield forecasts. Nevertheless, with Fed policy uncertainty 
easing, we see an opportunity to use the equity market dip to 
add exposure amid solid corporate earnings.  
Fed moves to restore credibility:  The Fed’s policy pivot this 
week followed Chair Warsh’s hawkish Jackson Hole speech 
and recent economic data prints, namely: a pick -up in job 
creation and inflation over the summer and a sharp rebound in 
oil prices due to the re -escalation of the Middle E ast conflict. 
Given the unanimous Fed decision to hike rates this week, we 
expect two more hikes by  June 2027 on the back of  robust 
growth amid strong AI investments and a tight labour market, 
keeping inflation elevated. We expect this pivot to help the Fed 
rebuild credibility as an inflation fighter.  
Policy outlook less aggressive than market pricing : The 
updated Fed policy rate estimates are lower compared with 
money market pricing of three more rate hikes by June 2027 
since we expect disinflation to take hold by Q2 2027 as the 
impact of oil prices and tariffs fade. We believe policy rates can 
return to the 4.0–4.25% range by end-2027, with the possibility 
of cuts in H2 2027 as inflation pressures ease.  
Upgrading near-term US rates and USD estimates : Based 
on these revised estimates, we also upgrade our 3-month target 
for the US 10 -year government bond yield to 5.0 -5.25%. We 
also revise up our 12 -month target for the US 10 -year bond 
yield to 4.75-5.0% (see page 4).  
Buying the equity dip as focus turns to fundamentals: Fed 
hikes typically do not hurt equities over the medium-term when 
accompanied by strong earnings growth. We estimate a 25bps 
rise in Fed rates (the discount rate) would impact the S&P500 
index and global equities by 3 -4%. However, we expect the 
headwind from higher rates to be offset by strong earnings 
growth, driving positive performance for global equities.  
Furthermore, a hawkish Fed is largely priced in and we expect 
the Fed to deliver fewer rate hikes than currently priced by 
markets. Meanwhile, we believe concerns over AI industry 
leaders slowing the pace of development are overdone. Hence, 
we maintain our preference for a diversified group of large cap 
technology and semiconductor industry leaders (see page 5-6). 
BoJ hikes; modestly bullish JPY : The BoJ hiked rates by 
25bps to a three-decade high of 1.25%, as expected, but the 7-
2 split vote led to renewed JPY weakness on concerns it may 
slow the pace of further hikes. We remain constructive on JPY 
amid narrowing rate differential vs peers as the BoJ potentially 
hikes rates by 25bps once per quarter until Q2 2027 to curb 
rising domestic wage-driven inflation pressure . Any sign of BoJ 
slowing the pace of hikes could lead to renewed JPY weakness, 
fuelling inflationary pressures (see page 7).  
BoE holds; rangebound GBP: The BoE held rates this week, 
bucking the hiking trend among peers, as weakening UK job 
and housing markets offset rebound in energy-driven inflation. 
We expect BoE to deliver fewer rate hikes than the 100bps of 
increase currently priced by markets  as the job market slows 
further. This is likely to keep GBP rangebound (see page 7).  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya

### Page 4

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 18 September 2026

Top client questions 
  Where do you see US government bond  yields following 
the Fed’s 16 September decision to hike interest rates? 
Our view: The US 10-year government bond yield is likely to 
retest the 5.00-5.25% range in the near term. We favour 3-7-year 
maturity bond s in t he US, preferring credit over government 
bonds. We are looking for an opportunity to extend duration 
approaching Q2 2027. 
Rationale: Over the next few months, we expect 2- and 30-year US 
government bond yields to remain rangebound, but the 10-year yield 
is expected to rebound up to the 5.00-5.25% range. The reasons for 
this include: 1) oil prices staying elevated and potentially rise further 
due to the fluid geopolitical situation in the Middle East, with Saudi 
Arabia’s East-West oil pipeline shut, the Houthis now firmly in control 
of the Strait of Bab-al-Mandab, the Strait of Hormuz still closed and 
China returning as a buyer of crude oil in the market ; 2) despite 
notable AI leaders expressing the need to slow AI development, AI 
capex plans are showing no signs of slowing down, which continues 
to drive above-trend growth in the economy. This, in turn, also means 
AI-related heavy bond issuance  is expected to continue. These 
issuers typically issue long-dated tenors, and this competition for 
capital will also exert upward pressure on the US 10-year yields. 
We note that the US 10-year bond yield has room to move lower, 
driven by short-covering activities in the very near term as net-short 
positions had moved to extreme levels before th is week’s  Fed 
meeting. However, we think such downshifts would be short-lived 
and the central tendency for the 10-year yield would be to move back 
up to the 5.00-5.25% range. 
Meanwhile, we see US 30-year yields continuing to reflect the dire 
fiscal outlook for the US government. We expect US 2-year yields 
to continue trading in a range that reflects expectations of further 
federal funds rate hikes. For the record, we only expect two more 
rate hikes from the Fed into the middle of 2027 , while money 
markets are pricing in three more for the same period. 
Going into H2 2027, we expect oil prices to moderate (either 
through a resolution of the M iddle East conflict or a re-routing of oil 
supply chains)  and the inflationary effects from tariffs to fade. 
Inflation by then is likely be close to – if not at – the Fed’s target of 
2%. At this point, we see room for the Fed to contemplate rate 
cuts. US 2-year yields (the tenor that is most sensitive to Fed policy 
changes) will likely start to price th is in. With oil prices and inflation 
cooling, 10-year yields would also have room to come down. 
US 30-year yields, on the other hand, are expected to  remain 
elevated as the US fiscal deficit remains substantial next year and 
could become much worse than its already dire state today. As a 
result, the ‘term premium ’ may keep the ultra -long end elevated. 
Short of some unorthodox policy to artificially cap them lower, 30 -
year yields could continue to drift higher into H2 2027. 
We continue to favour the 3-7-year segment of the yield curve and 
prefer credit over government bond s. We see an opportunity for 
us to extend duration closer to Q2 2027. 
—  Jonathan Liang, CFA, CIO for Fixed Income and FX

We have updated our three- and 12-month outlook 
for US government bond yields

### Page 5

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 18 September 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Will the step -up in the  Fed’s risk-free rate trigger a re -
pricing of equities? 
Our view: We expect strong corporate earnings growth to offset 
the headwind of higher rates , driving positive performance for 
global equities. We remain Overweight global equities. 
Rationale: The Fed’s latest 25bps rate hike had been anticipated 
by the bond market, partly reflected in the bond yield  rise we have 
seen so far in 2026. Using the US 10-year government bond yield as 
a proxy for the risk -free rate, the yield has risen by 80-85bps this 
year. We estimate that each 25bps rise in the discount rate presents 
a valuation drag on global equities. On this basis, the rise in yields 
represents a potential 10-14% headwind for equities this year. 
However, the Fed’s hike reflects a solid expansion of US economic 
activity, resilient domestic spending and robust capital investment. 
This is consistent with the positive earnings revisions we have seen 
this year, with  2026 earnings growth projections now at 34% (and 
16% for 2027). Global equities are up 14% year-to-date (YTD), but 
even with a 14% valuation headwind from higher yields, we believe 
they can rise further, given strong earnings growth in 2026 and 2027. 
—  Fook Hien Yap, Senior Investment Strategist 
  What is the outlook for the US 10-year government bond 
yield? What is your tactical view on the DXY? 
Our view: We raise our three-month US 10 -year government 
bond yield forecast to the 5.00-5.25% range. Meanwhile, we see 
a near-term upward bias in the DXY after its recent downtrend, 
supported by recent Fed tightening and higher  short-term US 
bond yields.  
Rationale: The Fed 's decision  to raise the target range for the 
federal funds rate by 25bps to 3.75-4.00% validates the upside policy 
risks we flagged recently, following Fed Chair Warsh's Jackson Hole 
speech and a run of firmer US economic data. We expect growth 
to remain robust, the labour market  to remain tight and inflation to 
stay elevated, supporting one more hike before year-end 2026 and 
potentially another in H1 2027  – fewer than the three additional 
hikes currently priced in by money markets . For the US 10-year 
government bond yield, further policy tightening and lingering US 
fiscal concerns should keep yields elevated, but the Fed’s visible 
response to inflation should limit additional upward pressure, leaving 
the 10-year yield holding around the 5.00-5.25% range. 
Crucially, Warsh provided no forward guidance on further rate 
hikes. Markets had already priced in substantial additional rate hikes 
ahead of the Fed meeting, reducing the scope for further USD upside 
purely from rate repricing. Meanwhile, we expect further policy 
tightening from the ECB and the BoJ, while the RBA also remains 
biased towards further tightening. This should gradually narrow the 
US rate advantage and limit the extent of a DXY rebound. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Anthony Naab, CFA, Investment Strategist

Global equities’ earnings growth estimates have 
been revised higher this year, offsetting the 
headwind from higher rates

### Page 6

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 18 September 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  How should investors position in technology as AI lab 
CEOs call for a slowdown in frontier AI model development? 
Our view: We believe concerns over “pacing the frontier” are 
overdone. We maintain our preference to remain invested in 
and diversified across Big Tech and semiconductor leaders. 
Rationale: We believe it is prudent for investors to avoid 
overreacting to the recent calls for a slowdown in frontier AI model 
development, as they focus on responsible AI development, rather 
than halting AI model development or reducing spending. The global 
AI leadership  race, reinforced by the US and China’s  strategic 
ambitions, also makes a coordinated slowdown unlikely. 
While the near-term narrative may shift from AI training beneficiaries 
towards inference plays, hyperscalers continue to signal strong AI 
infrastructure commitments and improving monetisation, supporting 
our AI capex outlook. Rising AI adoption and agentic use cases 
should sustain inference demand, even if training activity moderates, 
benefiting custom AI chips, NAND flash memory and hyperscalers.  
We believe the decline in valuations  this year more than 
compensates for any downside risk to AI capex. Our Opportunistic 
idea on Global Semiconductors further reflects our conviction, with 
attractive valuations supporting selective additions during volatility. 
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist 
  With ASEAN and UK equities outperforming AI-focused 
markets in H2 2026, is it worth adding to these markets? 
Our view: ASEAN and UK equities offer portfolio diversification, 
but we are Underweight both markets in favour of US and Asia 
ex-Japan (AxJ) equities, given their stronger earnings potential. 
Rationale: The ASEAN and UK markets have outperformed the 
MSCI ACWI over the past three months, but both have 
underperformed on a YTD basis. Financials is the largest sector in 
the ASEAN (53%) and the UK (28%) and provides steady earnings. 
However, both markets have minimal exposure to the technology 
sector (just 3% and 1% , respectively), ultimately dragging down 
growth. Hence, while defensive markets offer value by increasing 
diversification and reducing volatility in a portfolio, we maintain our 
Underweight allocation for ASEAN and the UK, as we see much 
stronger earnings potential elsewhere in the US and AxJ markets.  
We remain Overweight US and AxJ, supported by robust earnings 
growth, as  AI still has the potential to become a major economic 
growth driver . Our 12-month S&P500 Index forecast of 8,400  
implies low-teens upside, supported by a strong earnings backdrop 
of 25-30% earnings-per-share growth for 2026 and 15 -20% for 
2027. AxJ also boasts the strongest earnings growth among major 
markets, fuelled by the AI semiconductor super-cycle. 
—  Cindy Lam, CFA, Senior Investment Strategist

Our AI capex estimates are 15-20% below 
consensus expectations to account for execution 
risks 
Global AI capex is expected to register a CAGR of 
33% over 2025-30

Source: Company reports, Standard Chartered

supported by robust earnings growth

### Page 7

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 18 September 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Why is it still appropriate to have a positive stance on US 
high-yield (HY) bonds? 
Our view: We remain opportunistically bullish on US HY bonds. 
Lower sensitivity to interest rate volatility, a below-trend default 
rate and a maturity wall pushed out to 2028-29 drive this view. 
Rationale: Recent interest rate volatility has highlighted a key 
structural advantage of US HY bonds: their higher coupon rates  
and shorter tenors relative to Developed Market (DM) government 
bonds and US investment-grade (IG) corporate bonds translate into 
a much lower sensitivity to interest rates, cushioning bond prices 
during yield backup episodes, where bond yields rise sharply. 
YTD, US HY has delivered 1.8% in total returns, outperforming DM 
government bonds and US IG corporate bonds by over 3% each on 
a relative basis. This outperformance was particularly evident during 
the recent back-up in bond yields, as inflation concerns re-emerged 
after renewed Middle East tensions – an environment where HY's 
lower rate sensitivity provided a relative cushion  even as broader 
fixed income markets came under pressure in absolute terms. On 
the fundamentals side, US HY corporate credit quality remains 
resilient. Default rates continue to track below long-run trend levels, 
and the maturity wall has been pushed out to 2028 -29, reducing 
near-term refinancing risk. Against a non -recessionary macro 
backdrop, we expect these credit fundamentals to hold, supporting 
the case for continued opportunistic exposure to the asset class.  
—  Ray Heung, Senior Investment Strategist 
  Do the latest BoJ and BoE meetings alter your view on the 
central banks’ rate paths and their corresponding currencies? 
Our view: We remain constructive on the JPY and expect two 
BoJ rate hikes this year and potentially one hike per quarter in 
H1 2027 as policy normalisation continues. Meanwhile, the BoE 
held rates at 3.75%. We expect GBP/USD to stay rangebound 
within 1.32-1.36 over the next 1-3 months, with downside risk 
towards the lower end of our medium-term 1.30-1.36 range. 
Rationale: Persistent inflation, higher energy prices and earlier JPY 
weakness support further BoJ policy normalisation . Continued 
hiking should progressively narrow the US -Japan rate gap, reduce 
the attractiveness of JPY -funded carry trades and support  the JPY 
over the medium term towards the 153-155 range. However, with a 
September hike largely priced  in, future tightening ’s pace  will be 
more important for the JPY. Meanwhile, the BoE was split 6-3 in its 
decision to hold rates at 3.75% , with three members preferring a 
25bps hike, signalling elevated inflation risks. However, a weaker 
labour market is likely to limit the pace of BoE tightening. The US-
UK rate divergence is expected to favour the USD over the next 1-3 
months. Further BoE tightening (if inflation remains elevated ) could 
narrow the rate gap and provide support to the GBP later in the cycle. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist

We expect US HY bonds to continue delivering 
strong performance in the near term

DM government bond indices

## Document 202: AIがサイバー攻撃を自動化する時代、サイバー株は上がるのか ～攻撃能力の急伸と企業業績・株価から検証する～

Source PDF: all report pdf/AIがサイバー攻撃を自動化する時代、サイバー株は上がるのか ～攻撃能力の急伸と企業業績・株価から検証する～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-06
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Research Report

2026 年 10 月 6 日

1/6 
AI がサイバー攻撃を自動化する時代、サイバー株
は上がるのか 
～攻撃能力の急伸と企業業績・株価から検証する～ 
 資産運用調査部 主席研究員  柏村 祐

（要旨） 
○ AI によるサイバー攻撃の自動化・高度化が現実のものとなっている。豪政府システムへのAI
侵入事件などが示すように、AI は与えられた目的を達成する過程で、許可された範囲を超え
てシステムにアクセスしうることが明らかになった。国内でも 9 月下旬以降、幅広い業種の
企業で不正アクセスの公表が相次いでいる。 
○ AI エージェントのサイバー課題解決能力はここ数年で急速に向上しており、サイバーセキュ
リティ企業の売上高・利益も足元で好調に推移している。 
○ 一方で、高度な AI モデルの登場は既存のサイバーセキュリティ製品の陳腐化リスクへの懸
念を生み、関連株が下落するというパラドックスも起きている。「サイバー株なら何でも上
がる」という安易な連想買いには注意が必要である。 
○ 個人投資家は、サイバーセキュリティセクター全体を丸ごと買うのではなく、「AIネイティ
ブ」なサイバーセキュリティ企業や、AIを活用した防衛技術を持つ巨大テック企業（メガテ
ック）への選別投資が求められる。

1. 豪政府システムへの「AI侵入事件」が鳴らす警鐘 
2026 年9月、オーストラリアのアルバニージー首相は、同国の公的医療保険制度（Medicare）
の統計ポータルサイトに、OpenAIのAIエージェントが無断で侵入していたと発表した。注目すべ
きは、これが悪意あるハッカーによるサイバー攻撃ではなく、OpenAIの研究チームがAIに調査を
実行させる過程で起きた点だ。AIは調査中にアクセス制限に直面すると、自ら制限を回避する方
法を見つけ出し、非公開のファイルにまでアクセスしたという。この事例は、AIが与えられた目
的を達成する過程で、許可された範囲を超えてシステムにアクセスしうることを示した。 
サイバー攻撃の脅威は、海の向こうだけの話ではない。日本でも9月下旬以降、物流、交通、
小売、カーシェアリングなど幅広い業種の企業が、不正アクセスによる個人情報流出の可能性を
相次いで公表している。これらの攻撃にAIが用いられたかどうかは明らかになっていないが、サ
イバー攻撃の影響が業種を問わず及んでいることは確かだ。政府も10月1 日から、能動的サイバ

### Page 2

2/6 
ー防御の一環として、重大なサイバー攻撃を未然に防ぐため、警察や自衛隊が攻撃元のサーバー
等にアクセスし、無害化措置を講じることを可能にした。 
AIがサイバー攻撃を自動化・高度化させる時代が到来しつつある。企業や政府の防御需要は急
増し、サイバーセキュリティ企業には特需がもたらされると期待される。個人投資家の間の「AI
の次なる恩恵銘柄はサイバー株」という見方にも、一定の説得力がある。しかし、 「サイバー株な
ら何でも上がる」と考えるのは早計である。AIによる攻撃の自動化・高度化は、攻撃件数を増や
すだけでなく、従来のセキュリティ製品や防御手法の有効性そのものを揺るがしかねないから
だ。本稿では、AIの攻撃能力の現在地と、それがサイバーセキュリティ企業の業績および株価に
与える影響を、データに基づいて検証する。そのうえで、 「サイバー株＝安全確実な成長株」とい
う見方をどう見直すべきか、個人投資家に向けて投資戦略を考えるための新たな視点を提示した
い。

2. AIの攻撃能力急伸とサイバーセキュリティ企業の業績・株価への影響 
(1) AIは人間なしでどこまで攻撃できるのか 
AIエージェントのサイバー攻撃関連能力は急速に高まっている。スタンフォード大学人間中心
AI研究所（Stanford HAI）が公表した「AI Index Report 2026」によれば、AIは人間が逐次指示
を与えなくても、専門的なサイバー課題を自律的に解ける水準に達しつつある。 
同レポートで紹介されている「Cybench」は、暗号、Webセキュリティ、リバースエンジニアリ
ング、フォレンジック、エクスプロイトなど、40の専門レベルのサイバーセキュリティ課題を評
価するベンチマークである。資料1 は、人間のガイドなし（unguided）でAIが課題を解決できた
割合の推移を示している。これを見ると、2024年時点ではわずか15％であった解決率が、2025年
には 82％へと急伸し、2026年には93％に達している。2年間で 78ポイントの上昇であり、AIが
自律的にサイバー課題を解く能力が大きく向上していることがわかる。

### Page 3

3/6 
資料 1 AI エージェントの自律的なサイバー課題解決能力は急伸している 
（注）サイバーセキュリティのベンチマーク「Cybench」における、人間のガイドなし（unguided）での課題解決率（％）の推移。
Cybench は暗号、Web セキュリティ、リバースエンジニアリング、フォレンジック、エクスプロイトなど 40 の専門レベル課題を評価
するもの。 
（出所）Stanford HAI, “AI Index Report 2026”をもとに第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

(2) サイバー需要は企業の売上・利益に届いているのか 
こうしたAIによるサイバー攻撃の脅威が高まるなか、防御側であるサイバーセキュリティ企業
への需要拡大が期待される。実際、その需要はすでに企業業績に表れている。 
Nasdaqが公表した「Q1 2026 HXRXL Cybersecurity Commentary」によれば、サイバーセキュリ
ティ関連銘柄で構成される「Nasdaq ISE Cyber Security Select Index（HXRXL） 」構成企業の
2026 年第1 四半期決算は極めて好調であった。同レポートによると、構成企業の売上高は前年同
期比で 11％増加し、純利益は同29.3％の増加を記録しており、売上高以上に純利益が大きく伸び
ていることが確認できる。さらに、市場予想に対する結果をまとめた資料2を見ても、売上高で
予想を上回った企業は指数ウェイトの92.2％を占め、純利益に至っては100％の企業が予想を上
回った。サイバーセキュリティ需要の強さは、売上高と利益の双方に表れている。

### Page 4

4/6 
資料 2 サイバーセキュリティ企業の業績は市場予想を大きく上回る 
（注）Nasdaq ISE Cyber Security Select Index（HXRXL）構成企業の 2026 年第 1 四半期決算の集計結果。市場予想に対する売上
高および純利益の予想超え・予想未達の企業数（指数ウェイトベース）、ならびに平均上振れ・下振れ率（％）を示す。 
（出所）Nasdaq, “Q1 2026 HXRXL Cybersecurity Commentary”をもとに第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

(3) それでもサイバー株は上がるのか 
業績が好調であれば株価も上昇すると考えるのが自然だが、 「AIによる攻撃高度化＝サイバー株
上昇」とは必ずしも言い切れない。AIは防御需要を増やす一方で、既存のサイバーセキュリティ
製品を陳腐化させるとの懸念も生んでいるからだ。 
国際決済銀行（BIS）が2026 年7月に公表したレポート「A Mythos moment? Frontier AI and 
cyber risk」は、このパラドックスを指摘している。2026年 4月にAnthropicが高度なサイバー攻
撃能力を持つAIモデル「Mythos」を発表した際、サイバーセキュリティ関連株は大きく下落し
た。資料3 は、Mythos発表前を0とした場合のサイバーセキュリティ関連株の累積異常リターン
（CAR）を示している。発表後、サイバーセキュリティETF（IHAK）や主要企業（CRWD、PANWな
ど）のCARは軒並みマイナスに転じ、一部の系列ではグラフ上で約−30％の大幅なマイナスを記録
した。BISは、この市場反応について「新しいAIモデルの登場により、既存のサイバーセキュリテ
ィ製品やサービスが陳腐化する（obsolescence）という投資家の懸念を反映している」との解釈
を紹介している。つまり、AIの進化はサイバーセキュリティ市場全体を拡大させる可能性が高い
ものの、同時にゲームチェンジを引き起こし、旧来の技術やサービスに依存する企業の競争力を
急速に奪うリスクを孕んでいる。

### Page 5

5/6 
資料 3 高度な AI モデルの発表後、サイバーセキュリティ関連株は下落した 
（注）2026 年 4 月の高度な AI モデル「Mythos」発表前後の、サイバーセキュリティ関連株の累積異常リターン（CAR、％）。発表前
を 0 として推移を示している。「Glasswing partners」は CrowdStrike（CRWD）および Palo Alto Networks（PANW）を指す。 
（出所）Bank for International Settlements, “BIS Bulletin No. 129: A Mythos moment? Frontier AI and cyber risk”をもとに第一ラ
イフ資産運用経済研究所作成

3. AI時代のサイバー株投資～「陳腐化リスク」を回避する新戦略～ 
第 2 章で見てきたように、AI はサイバー攻撃能力を飛躍的に高めている。また、足元のサイバ
ーセキュリティ企業の業績も好調である。しかし、最先端のAIモデルが登場した際には、市場は
既存のサイバーセキュリティ製品の「陳腐化リスク」に敏感に反応し、関連株は大きく売り込ま
れた。ここからいえるのは、サイバーセキュリティ市場全体は今後も拡大が見込まれるものの、
企業間の「勝ち負け」はこれまで以上に鮮明になるということだ。こうした環境変化のなか、個
人投資家には従来の投資アプローチを根本から見直すことが求められる。 
第一に、サイバーセキュリティセクター全体を丸ごと買うアプローチ（テーマ型ETFやインデ
ックスファンドなど）には注意が必要である。市場全体が成長しても、AIの進化に取り残され、
陳腐化して淘汰される旧世代の企業をポートフォリオに抱え込むリスクが高まるからだ。足元の
業績や過去の市場シェアだけで安心することはできない。第二に、個別銘柄を選別する際は、 「AI
ネイティブ」なサイバーセキュリティ企業であるかどうかが最大の焦点となる。攻撃側がAI を駆
使する以上、防御側もAIをシステムの中心に据え、未知の脅威を自律的に検知・遮断できるプラ
ットフォームを持っていなければ対抗できない。レガシーな製品を販売している企業ではなく、
クラウドベースで膨大なデータをAI で解析し、防御機能を継続的に高度化できる企業を見極める
必要がある。第三に、サイバー防衛の投資対象を「セキュリティ専業企業」以外にも広げること
である。 
最先端のAI モデルを開発し、強固なクラウドインフラを持つ巨大テック企業（メガテック）
は、自社のAI を活用した防衛技術を、顧客向けの強力なセキュリティサービスとして提供し始め

### Page 6

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
6/6 
ている。豊富な資金力とAI 開発力を持つメガテックを、サイバー防衛の担い手として評価し、ポ
ートフォリオの中核に据えることも、AI時代の投資戦略として有効な選択肢となるだろう。

## Document 205: AI交易何时止跌？市场盯着Meta“天量发债”背后的“囚徒困境”

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI交易何时止跌？市场盯着Meta“天量发债”背后的“囚徒困境”.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-25
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

巴克莱认为， AI 巨头争相提前融资形成 “ 囚徒困境 ” ，集体发债将推⾼融资成本，限制信⽤利
差收窄。随着低评级债券⻛险上升、 AI 发⾏⼈信⽤分化加剧，融资供给能否被市场消化，
将成为判断 AI 交易能否企稳的关键。
摘要：
过去⼀周， AI 交易遭遇新⼀轮抛售压⼒，美国股票、债券市场同步承压。纳斯达克出现数⽉来最
差单⽇表现，美国⾼收益债⻛险溢价升⾄ 17 个⽉⾼位， CCC 级垃圾债收益率则攀升⾄ 2020 年以
来最⾼⽔平。股指表⾯波动有限，底层资产的⻛险重估却在加速。
引发市场担忧的焦点之⼀，是 Meta 在公布三季度业绩后释放的巨额融资计划。据巴克莱 10 ⽉ 23
⽇发布的研究报告， Meta 选择提前融资，部分原因是 AI ⽣态内其他企业已陆续启动或计划启动
融资，资本市场的吸纳能⼒正变得越来越有限。
这揭⽰了 AI 巨头⾯临的 “ 囚徒困境 ” ：每家公司都有抢先融资的动机，但如果巨头集体加快发
债，新增供给就可能推⾼融资成本，反过来挤压所有参与者的融资空间。与此同时，对冲基⾦

持续削减杠杆， AI 相关信⽤资产的利差分化也在扩⼤。
AI 交易何时⽌跌，因⽽不只取决于科技巨头的盈利表现，还取决于市场能否消化密集的债券供
给，以及不断上升的融资成本是否会进⼀步冲击 AI 产业链。
股指表⾯平静， AI 仓位遭集中清理
过去⼀周，指数表现掩盖了个股之间的剧烈分化。⾼盛数据显⽰，其 AI 受益股与⻛险股多空组
合较 6 ⽉⾼点下跌约 55% ，年内涨幅悉数回吐；动量策略也出现⼤幅回撤，跌⾄ 2008 年⾦融危机
以来的极端⽔平。
周四，标普 500 指数下跌 1.2% ，仍有 335 只成份股上涨；等权重标普 500 指数上涨 0.7% ，市值加
权的 SPY 却收跌。⾼盛数据显⽰，对冲基⾦在此前⼋个交易⽇中有七天进⾏去杠杆操作，周四
的抛售完全由多头平仓驱动。
这表明，近期调整不仅是对 AI 估值的重新审视，也涉及拥挤仓位的集中出清。但仓位调整能否
带来持续反弹，仍取决于信⽤市场压⼒是否缓解。

### Page 2

信⽤⻛险向低评级借款⼈集中
据彭博，美国⾼收益债平均⻛险溢价升⾄ 17 个⽉⾼位，⼀级市场出现年内最⼤规模的撤单潮；
CCC 级垃圾债收益率升⾄ 15.85% ，创 2020 年以来新⾼，与 BB 级债券之间的利差也逼近 2016 年
和 2020 年的峰值。
杠杆贷款市场同样承压。价格低于⾯值 60 美分的深度困境贷款规模升⾄ 2020 年 3 ⽉以来最⾼⽔
平。摩根⼤通数据显⽰，过去 12 个⽉，价格在⾯值 80 美分及以下的贷款规模激增近 90% 。
值得注意的是，CCC 级债券利差⼤幅⾛阔之际，整体信⽤市场并未同步恶化，说明⻛险正集中
于偿债能⼒较弱的发⾏⼈。DoubleLine Capital 企业信⽤总监 Robert Cohen 指出，部分 CCC 级
借款⼈可能⾛向债务重组，但这并不意味着整个信⽤周期已经逆转。
⾼盛数据显⽰，从投资级、⾼收益信⽤衍⽣品到低评级现⾦债券，多个评级区间的利差均触及
本轮周期⾼位。 CreditSights 新加坡亚太公司信⽤主管 R. Lakshmanan 认为，多重全球⻛险接连

冲击市场后，信⽤⻛险重新定价正逐渐形成持续趋势。
Meta 发债揭⽰ AI 融资 “ 囚徒困境 ”
Meta 的融资计划将 AI 资本开⽀与信⽤市场供给之间的⽭盾推到了台前。公司将 2026 年资本开⽀
预期收窄⾄ 1350 亿⾄ 1450 亿美元，预计第四季度⽀出达 490 亿⾄ 590 亿美元；分红后的⾃由现⾦
流转负，管理层还预计从今年秋季开始融资数千亿美元。
巴克莱在报告中指出，超⼤规模科技公司的营收增⻓正在兑现，但资本开⽀仍持续攀升，融资
问题已从 “ 能否融到资 ” 转向 “ 何时融、融多少、如何融 ” 。
Meta 在申报⽂件中表⽰，鉴于 AI ⽣态内其他企业已启动或计划启动融资，资本市场的吸纳能⼒
正变得越来越有限，因此提前融资具有现实必要性。
但当所有公司都选择抢先融资，个体的理性决策就可能转化为整体市场的压⼒：新增债券供给
集中涌⼊市场，推⾼发⾏成本，并限制信⽤利差收窄。为避免过度举债损害信⽤评级，企业也
开始考虑股权融资、可转债、资产⽀持证券（ ABS ）及表外融资等渠道。

巴克莱认为， AI 相关融资供给将在短期内继续限制信⽤利差收窄空间。
AI 信⽤分化加剧，市场转向个券定价

2 条评论
巴克莱估算， AI 相关信⽤资产规模约为 6800 亿美元，涵盖超⼤规模科技公司、半导体、数据中
⼼及硬件企业，占投资级债券指数市值约 7.5% ，却贡献约 13% 的指数久期⻛险敞⼝（ DTS ）。
整体⽽⾔， AI 信⽤资产相较投资级指数的利差溢价约为 35 个基点，期限超过 10 年的债券溢价更
达到约 55 个基点。与此同时， AI 发⾏⼈之间的利差离散度过去⼀年从 24 个基点升⾄ 65 个基点；
即使剔除甲⻣⽂和数据中⼼板块，该指标仍上升约 60% ，达到 37 个基点。
⾃ 9 ⽉中旬以来，甲⻣⽂、 Meta 、 SpaceX 及多家数据中⼼运营商的信⽤利差扩张幅度明显超过
指数；微软、⾕歌和亚⻢逊的短期限债券则相对抗压。市场开始根据发⾏⼈的融资需求、资产

负债表和⻓期⻛险进⾏区分，⽽⾮将 AI 视为统⼀的信⽤主题。
收益率曲线也反映出⻓端压⼒。巴克莱数据显⽰，过去⼀年，超⼤规模科技公司的平均 2 年期⾄
5 年期、 5 年⾄ 10 年期及 10 年⾄ 30 年期收益率曲线分别陡峭化 137% 、 140% 和 68% 。其中， Meta
的 10 年⾄ 30 年期曲线利差达到 68 个基点，在同类发⾏⼈中最为陡峭。
⻓端曲线⾛陡，反映投资者对⻓期债务要求的⻛险补偿上升。随着 AI 企业持续扩⼤资本开⽀，市
场不仅要判断投资能否转化为收⼊，还要评估融资规模、现⾦流改善时间及新增债券对融资成
本的影响。
因此， AI 交易能否⽌跌，关键在于巨额融资能否被市场消化、信⽤利差能否企稳，以及 AI 企业
之间的⻛险分化是否继续扩⼤。在这些压⼒缓解之前，阶段性反弹仍可能受到融资成本上升的
制约。

## Document 244: Compelling risk-reward in US 10-year bonds

Source PDF: all report pdf/Compelling risk-reward in US 10-year bonds.pdf
Report source: 渣打銀行 (Market Watch)
Date: 2026-09-28
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Compelling risk-reward in 
US 10-year bonds  
Summary 
Event: The US 10 -year government bond yield has surged 
past 5.0% to a 19-year high and has now entered our three -
month forecast range of 5.0-5.25%. At the time of writing, the 
10-year yield is trading at 5.2%, its highest since June 2007’s 
high of 5.32%. The next resistance levels are the 2002 high 
of 5.47% and the 2001 high of 5.53%.  
We view the risk/reward for the US 10-year government 
bond after the recent yield spike as  compelling and we 
are initiating a long opportunistic idea at the current 
yield. As the table on the right shows, if the 10 -year yield 
rises to 6%, investors would face less than 1% in negative 
total returns over a one-year period, whereas if the yield falls 
below 5.0%, they would gain at least 6% in total returns.  
While we continue to prefer the 3-7-year bond maturity range 
for income generation (the US 5-year bond yield is also 
above 5%), and broadly prefer corporate over government 
bonds amid solid corporate fundamentals, the US 10 -year 
government bond yield has finally reached reasonable levels 
for investors looking for slightly longer maturities. 
Rationale: We expect the US 10-year government bond yield 
to ease to 4.75-5.0% over a 12-month period due to: 
Easing oil prices and inflation over next 12 months: While 
the yield is likely to remain elevated in the next three months 
due to elevated oil prices caused by geopolitical uncertainty, 
heavy AI investment-driven bond issuance and a resilient US 
economy, it is likely to ease towards the 4.75 -5.0% range 
over 12  months as oil prices moderate either due to a 
resolution in the Middle East or a rerouting of Middle East 
energy supplies and as the impact of tariffs fade.  
We expect s hallower Fed rate hikes than market s: We 
expect a total of 50bps of Fed rate hikes by June 2027, before 
a cut in H2 as US inflation eventually eases towards the Fed’s 
2% target. This  rate trajectory is shallower than c. 95bps of 
further hikes priced by markets over the next 12 months. 
 The benchmark US 10-year government bond yield has 
risen to its highest since 2007, offering medium-term 
investors a compelling risk-reward trade-off

### Page 2

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 28 September 2026

Investor positioning adjustment: In the very near-term, the 
US 10 -year bond yield has room to move lower as hedge 
funds using systematic strategies (CTAs) are extremely short 
US government bonds, raising the chance of short -covering 
if upcoming jobs and inflation data miss estimates. 
A shallow Fed hiking cycle  
We expect the shallowest Fed hiking cycle in almost 
three decades. This should enable the US 10-year yield to 
ease towards 4.75 -5.0% over a 12 -month period. Following 
this month’s increase, we forecast two further 25bp hikes by 
June 2027, followed by a cut in H2 2027. Markets price at 
least three more hikes by June and a strong probability of a 
fourth by September. Our projected 75bp s of total hikes this 
cycle compares with 525bps of hikes in 2022–23, 225bps in 
2015–18, 425bps in 2004–06 and 175bp in 1999–2000. The 
last smaller mid-cycle move was a single 25bps hike in 1997. 
Why we expect a more cautious Fed 
The latest hike restored the Fed’s credibility after renewed 
oil-price pressures, elevated inflation and a balanced labour 
market prompted a unanimous policy vote. However, the 
following factors argue against aggressive tightening: 
Supply-driven inflation: Energy costs and AI-infrastructure 
constraints, rather than excess demand, are driving prices. 
Higher rates cannot repair supply and may weaken growth. 
Labour is no longer the main inflation source:  Labour 
supply and demand are better balanced, wage growth has 
moderated and unemployment is near full employment. AI 
further weakens the traditional Phillips Curve relationship 
between unemployment and inflation. 
Disinflation should resume:  Energy pressures may fade 
after the US mid-term elections, while the one-off inflationary 
effects of tariffs should diminish after 2025–26. 
AI raises the neutral rate , but dampens inflation:  
Investment and productivity may lift the neutral rate ( r*), the 
rate consistent with stable inflation and full employment, 
while efficiency gains limit wage-price spirals. 
Markets are already tightening  financial conditions : 
Higher bond yields have raised borrowing costs and 
restrained activity, doing part of the Fed’s work. 
The cycle began from a higher base:  The Fed funds rate 
started at 3.75%, versus 0.25% in the previous two cycles 
and 1% before that, reducing additional tightening required. 
Monetary transmission has weakened:  Wealthier 
households drive more spending but are less rate -sensitive, 
while lower -income consumers are already constrained. 
Further hikes may , therefore, curb demand less effectively 
while causing disproportionate harm to low-income families. 
 We expect a shallower Fed rate hiking cycle compared 
with market expectations

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 28 September 2026

Small business conditions are deteriorating:  Higher 
financing and energy costs are weakening sentiment, as 
reflected in the latest Beige Book . The renewed 
underperformance of US small capitalisation (S&P 600) and 
mid capitalisation (S&P 400) equity indices  versus the large 
cap S&P 500 index over the past couple of months also show 
rising pressure on small businesses. 
Fiscal and financial -stability risks are rising:  Aggressive 
Fed tightening would increase US debt-servicing costs as the 
US Treasury seeks cheaper funding through shorter-maturity 
issuance, while potentially amplifying financial-system stress. 
Upside risk to bond yields 
Our view of lower bond yields carries the following risks: 
Persistent inflation:  Repeated supply shocks, strong AI -
related investment demand and tighter immigration controls 
could keep inflation above target and force steeper Fed rate 
hikes. With unemployment near the Fed’s estimate of full 
employment, a sharp drop in labour supply could intensify 
wage pressures and destabilise inflation expectations . We 
note that long-term expectations remain contained for now. 
Geopolitical escalation:  An intensification of geopolitical 
conflicts after the mid -term elections could lift energy prices 
and inflation. However, a Democrat -controlled or divided 
Congress may constrain US foreign-policy initiatives through 
tighter budgets, while Republicans could turn less supportive 
as attention shifts to the 2028 presidential race. 
Higher neutral rate: AI investments could raise the neutral 
rate (r*), turning current monetary policy settings 
accommodative. This could necessitate further rate hikes. 
However, the Fed has barely raised its nominal neutral rate 
estimate, lifting it to 3.2% in September from 3.0% a year ago. 
Bottomline: The outlook for rates remains geopolitics 
dependent, with all Fed meetings live . However, based on 
our core scenario, we see the US 10-year yield settling lower 
in the 4.75-5.0% in 6-12 months. A shallow Fed hiking cycle 
due to expected disinflation offers investors a reasonably 
attractive risk-reward in US 10-year government bonds. 
US 30-year government bonds , on the other hand, may 
continue to see elevated yields as the US fiscal deficit is 
unlikely to have improved next year, and may look much 
worse. As a result, “term premium” may keep the ultra -long 
end elevated. Short of some unorthodox policy to artifici ally 
cap them lower, the 30-year yield is likely to continue to drift 
higher towards the 5.5-5.75% range into H2 2027. 
— Jonathan Liang, CIO for Fixed Income and FX   
Rajat Bhattacharya, Senior Investment Strategist 
Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist 
AI-related bond issuances are a key factor keeping 
US bond yields elevated in the near term; we oil and 
tariff effects on inflation to fade over 12 months, 
offsetting AI-driven effects on bond yields

## Document 271: Expected 2027 TMT supply points to a new record

Source PDF: all report pdf/Expected 2027 TMT supply points to a new record.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-21
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

European TMT gross bond supply may reach €150bn in 2027,
driven by tech funding for AI and data centres rather than
traditional telecoms. Strong cash ﬂows and credit quality
support issuance, but disciplined funding will be key to market
absorption
Jan Frederik Slijkerman
AI and data centre investment is expected to be the main driver of TMT bond
issuance in 2027
We predict another year of strong supply for the European TMT
sector. We expect €150bn of gross issuance and €103bn of net

issuance, given €47bn of debt redemptions. The additional
issuance is not coming from the traditional telecom or media
issuers, but from global tech companies.
As we expect the data centre build-out to continue in 2027, issuers
are likely to return to the European investment-grade credit market
to raise part of the required funding. Meanwhile, we do not see a
major reason for traditional sectors to reduce or increase

Telecoms supply
We will break down the main factors aﬀecting debt issuance,
starting with FY27 capex requirements. While we expect capex as a
percentage of sales to decline, aggregate capex is likely to increase

slightly. Companies such as Orange and Vodafone are integrating
recent acquisitions and may need to invest additional capital to
realise merger synergies. We therefore do not expect the lower
capex-to-sales ratio to translate into less issuance. Nevertheless,
BT and KPN are exceptions, as both clearly intend to reduce capex
in FY27.
Low-single-digit EBITDA growth gives companies some capacity to
issue additional debt while maintaining stable leverage. However,

higher interest rates provide an incentive to keep debt levels in
check. We therefore expect the increase in debt issuance to be
driven mainly by M&A. Several debt-funded acquisitions of smaller
networks could generate around €5bn of net issuance. A further
€3bn could come from US operators raising euro-denominated
funding for spectrum payments in 2027.

### Page 3

Technology supply
In 2027, we believe the AI build-out will continue. Tech companies
currently report healthy returns on investment, demand for AI
services continues to outpace supply, and companies want to
maintain their market positions. We therefore expect further capex
growth in 2027, with the large hyperscalers investing close to
US$1tr in ﬁscal 2027.
As in 2026, we expect these companies to ﬁnance their
investments largely from operating cash ﬂow, but also through

debt issuance, with additional funding coming from European
markets. We expect hyperscaler data centre operators to issue at
least €60bn in 2027, somewhat above the approximately €35bn
issued year-to-date in 2026, including issuance by NTT.
In addition to the €60bn of issuance from hyperscaler data centre
operators, we expect €35bn of net supply from other technology
issuers. Companies are likely to pursue acquisitions and raise
additional funding for investment, including semiconductor,

software and payment services companies. Including €8bn from
telecom issuers, this brings total TMT net supply to €103bn. This
would translate into gross supply of approximately €150bn, which,
in our view, is close to the amount the European market may be
able to absorb.
Supply discipline will be important
Companies will need to issue prudently and provide clear guidance
on future funding plans, as spreads may only tighten once the
market no longer expects substantial new supply. Nevertheless,

current spreads look attractive relative to the issuers’ high ratings,
with much of the widening driven by supply and liquidity eﬀects.
Relatively wide CDS spreads also suggest that technical factors,
rather than a material deterioration in credit risk, are weighing on
valuations. This combination of high credit quality and attractive
spreads should attract new money to the investment-grade
market.

Challenge 1: Does debt issuance reduce
credit quality?
First, we expect the hyperscaler data centre operators Amazon,
Microsoft, Alphabet, Meta and Oracle to grow their combined
EBITDA by US$230bn, or 29%, in FY27, to around US$1tr. Moreover,
with the exception of Oracle, these companies are expected to
operate with limited net debt, with Microsoft holding more cash
than debt. The main caveat is their signiﬁcant lease commitments,
many of which are not yet reﬂected in balance-sheet lease

rating agencies will attach greater weight to these liabilities,
particularly if returns on investment start to decline. In our view, this
is not a material concern for 2027 and may never become one,
given the sector’s strong growth and ability to fund a signiﬁcant
part of investment from operating cash ﬂow. Companies also retain
ﬂexibility to scale back future investment that has not yet been
committed if returns start to disappoint.
Challenge 2: Do credit benchmarks

accommodate more supply?
Do European credit markets have the capacity to absorb additional
supply from technology companies? We think the current share of
technology issuers remains modest. Alphabet accounts for only
1.16% of the iBoxx € Non-Financials index, while Amazon and IBM
account for 0.68% and 0.91%, respectively.
In the US, Oracle’s share of more than 2% in the iBoxx $ Domestic
Non-Financials index illustrates that individual technology issuers

can reach materially higher benchmark weights, while several high-

## Document 276: First Impressions_ NZ household incomes, assets and savings update - June quarter 2026 (2026-10-26)

Source PDF: all report pdf/First Impressions_ NZ household incomes, assets and savings update - June quarter 2026 (2026-10-26).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-26
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#consumer #newzealand
First Impressions: NZ household incomes, assets and savings
update - June quarter 2026
By Satish Ranchhod
Senior Economist, Westpac NZ
10:24 October 08 2026 Share
New Zealand incomes and savings are rising, as is the value of households’ financial
assets. However, the value of housing assets remains flat.
Key takeouts from Stats NZ’s latest update on household incomes, assets and savings (June quarter
2026)

### Page 2

Household incomes
Disposable income levels in the economy grew by 5.2% in the year to June, down from 6.5% in the
year to March

Savings and wealth
Household savings have continued to rise, climbing by $2.5b over the past quarter.

Overall household wealth levels were up 1.9% over the past year. Under the surface, the make-up of
household wealth continues to shift.

Interest costs 
Overall household income levels are continuing to rise. Much of that is due to increases in
entrepreneurial earnings (which is in part due to increased earnings in the agricultural sector). Wage
and salary growth has been more limited but has picked up.
Household savings levels have been rising for three years now, with another solid rise over the past
three months. 
Overall household wealth levels have risen modestly. Although the value of households’ financial
assets is continuing to rise, housing assets have essentially shown no growth since late 2023.
Household spending on interest costs rose just slightly in the June quarter. That’s the first increase in
two years. The easing in average borrowing costs seen over the past few years has come to a close
with the RBNZ’s hiking cycle now in train.
Underpinning the rise in overall income levels have been continued increases in entrepreneurial
earnings, which are up 12% over the past year. That will include stronger earnings in the agricultural
sector, which has been boosted by the strength in commodity export prices. That lift in agricultural
earnings has been reflected in stronger economic confidence and spending in many rural regions.
We expect commodity export earnings will remain firm over the year ahead. 
In contrast, wage and salary earnings are up just 2.7% over the past year. That’s up from 1.8% in the
year to March, but it’s a fairly muted rise, especially given the continued increases in the population
and inflation of 4.1%.
June’s large increase in savings builds on the solid increases seen over the past three years.
The savings rate in the June quarter was 3.7% of disposable incomes, down just slightly from last
quarter.
The value of households’ financial assets has continued to rise and is up 4.6% over the past year. 
However, the value of housing and land assets is down 0.9% over the past year, and it has remained
essentially unchanged since late 2023. We expect housing market conditions will remain soft over
the coming year, limiting increases in overall household wealth. 
Households’ spending on interest costs rose just slightly in the June quarter, climbing by 0.3%.
While that’s a relatively small change, it is the first increase that we’ve seen in two years.

### Page 3

Related articles

Media Contact:
Satish Ranchhod, Senior Economist
P: +64 9 336 5668
M: +64 21 710 852
Over the past few years, many borrowers saw their debt servicing costs fall as they rolled off earlier
high mortgage rates and onto lower ones. That helped to support household spending.
However, the easing in average borrowing cost has now ended. Recent months have seen mortgage
rates pushing higher as the RBNZ has begun to hike the Official Cash Rate, with further hikes
expected over the coming year. 
Looking ahead, we don’t expect a significant increase in average household borrowing costs.
However, we won’t see the same support for spending that we did over the past few years.

## Document 289: GLOBAL MARKETS CALL_ Bond Yields Ease

Source PDF: all report pdf/GLOBAL MARKETS CALL_ Bond Yields Ease.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-10-25
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: GLOBAL MARKETS CALL: Bond Yields Ease
Date:    3 hours ago
GLOBAL MARKETS CALL: Bond Yields Ease

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
GLOBAL MARKETS CALL: Bond
Yields Ease
By Ed Yardeni & Toby Hearst • 25 Oct 2026
View in browser
Photo by Jeffrey Hamilton / Unsplash

Bond yields eased in most major markets last week, but France was the
glaring exception. In stocks, the global AI selloff kept hitting the equities
markets that had gained the most this year, putting the US back ahead of
the All Country World ex-US ytd. That supports our downgrade last week
of Go Global to market weight, though the fundamental case for overseas
stocks remains strong. Here's more:
I. Global Bonds

### Page 2

Yields fell in five of the eight major bond markets we track (chart). The US
10-year Treasury yield fell to 5.00%. France's yield jumped 24bps to
5.02%, slightly surpassing the US’s. Yields also fell in the UK, Australia,
Germany, and Japan.
The spread between French and German 10-year yields widened 31bps
last week to 167.9bps (chart). Last week, we wrote that contagion from
France had eased for now. It’s back. Italy’s spread widened to 123.9bps

from 106.6bps. Greece’s widened to 105.0bps from 90.8bps. Italy’s 10-
year bond now yields 44bps less than France’s.
II. Global Stocks
Brazil remains at the top of the country ETF rankings mtd, up 14.3% in
dollars (chart). South Korea remains at the bottom, down 17.2%. The US

### Page 3

is down just 0.3%, while the All Country World ex-US ETF is down 2.8%.
Go Global no longer leads Stay Home this year. The US ETF is up 11.4%
ytd, ahead of the All Country World ex-US ETF's 9.5% (chart). Taiwan
(65.1%) and South Korea (55.6%) still top the ytd rankings, followed by
Brazil (34.1%). China (-15.6%) and Indonesia (-43.3%) are at the bottom.

### Page 4

III. Global Earnings & Valuations
The US MSCI's share of the All Country World MSCI's market cap has
edged up to 65.0% (chart). The Emerging Markets (EM) MSCI’s share
slipped to 11.7% from 12.1% last week.
Yet the EM MSCI now accounts for 20.2% of the All Country World MSCI’s
forward earnings (chart). That’s more than Europe’s 15.0%. It’s the first

### Page 5

time that EM has overtaken Europe since the data began in 1995. The US
accounts for 54.4% of forward earnings, well below its market-cap share.
That mismatch shows up in valuations. The US MSCI’s forward P/E is
19.2 versus 12.2 for the All Country World ex-US (chart). The ex-US
multiple has dropped from 15.7 in January even as its forward earnings
soared. The EM MSCI trades at just 9.0.
IV. In Focus
(1) Brazil. Brazil’s MSCI stock price index is up 22.2% ytd in local

currency and 31.0% in dollars (chart). The real has gained 8.0% against
the dollar this year. In dollar terms, the index remains less than half its
2008 peak. Stocks rallied after Senator Flávio Bolsonaro took a surprise
lead in the October 4 first round. He held a narrow edge in the final polls

### Page 6

before the October 25 runoff against President Luiz Inácio Lula da Silva. A
Bloomberg poll released on October 19 gave Bolsonaro 51.1% of valid
votes against 48.9% for Lula.
Valuations aren’t stretched. Brazil’s forward P/E is 9.2 (chart). That’s
below its 2025 high of 10.7. The catch is that analysts expect earnings to
grow 44.6% this year but just 0.6% in 2027.
Brazil’s central bank has room to ease. It has lowered the Selic rate to
13.75% from a peak of 15.00% (chart). Inflation was 4.6% in September.

That leaves a real policy rate of about 9%.

### Page 7

(2) China. China’s property bust is deepening. The Shenzhen Real Estate
Stock Price Index fell to 953.55, breaking below the floor that had held
since 2012 (chart). Last week, we wrote that we would worry if it did that.
Now we are worried. Real estate investment is down 20.6% through the
first nine months of the year. Exports continue to carry the economy.
Beijing’s stabilization efforts haven’t stopped the selling. The China ETF
(FXI) fell 4.5% last week. Chinese technology stocks have missed the AI

rally that lifted US tech (chart). The China Technology ETF (CQQQ) is at
40.26, far below its 2021 peak.

### Page 8

China’s bond market confirms the deflationary picture. The 10-year
government bond yield is down to 1.62% (chart). It’s one of only two major
yields that have fallen this year. China’s yield is now well below Japan’s
2.80%.

featured in our segment, "Ed Answers Your Questions".

121 亿美元）特别股利形式回馈股东，并拟于 10 ⽉ 28 ⽇三季报发布时正式宣布。
摘要：
SK 海⼒⼠据报将把出售铠侠股权所获巨额收益的⼤部分，以特别股利形式直接返还股东。
据韩国《东亚⽇报》援引投资银⾏业知情⼈⼠报道， SK 海⼒⼠计划派发约 16 万亿韩元（约 121
亿美元）特别股利，资⾦来源为出售⽇本存储芯⽚制造商铠侠（ Kioxia ）股权所获收益。
该计划预计将于 10 ⽉ 28 ⽇（周三） SK 海⼒⼠公布第三季财报时正式对外宣布。
据估计， SK 海⼒⼠在第⼀季及第⼆季陆续出售铠侠持股，扣除原始投资成本后，合计净利约达
20 万亿韩元（约合  151 亿美元）。

此次拟派发的 16 万亿韩元特别股利，约相当于上述净利的⼋成。
2018 年⻉恩资本牵头收购东芝存储 ( 即后来的铠侠 ) 时， SK 海⼒⼠通过两个 SPC( 特殊⽬的公司，
常⽤来隔离债务和法律⻛险 ) 分别买⼊了铠侠的股票和可转债。随着今年⻉恩卖出最后⼀批铠侠
股份，海⼒⼠也逐渐清仓了该公司的股票。
